#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server
-------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• $1.00 OFF any transaction when ANY LoyaltyID (e.g., phone #) is entered
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"   # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000             # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Hard-coded reward: $1.00 off transaction total (line 0)
DISCOUNT_VALUE = 1.00
REWARD_ID      = "DEMO-1OFF"
RECEIPT_SHORT  = "$1OFF"            # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG   = "Loyalty $1 Off"   # <= 24 chars

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""
    # Transaction-level reward (line 0), amountOff = $1.00, instant apply.
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>LOYALTY $1 OFF APPLIED</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>$1 Loyalty discount available</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply $1 off
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()


i have this code and i provided the documentation, can you go through the documentation and see if if there is a way to provide a 2 for, 3 for etc, type promotions, would it take it off the total? or per item?
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 1m 12s
Short answer: yes—you can do “2-for”, “3-for”, etc. by returning item-level rewards (per line) using RewardTargetLineNumber + RewardDiscountMethod + an optional RewardLimit. If you set RewardTargetLineNumber to 0, Passport treats it as a transaction-level discount (off the total). Otherwise it’s applied per item line (affecting that line’s price/amount). 
loyalty2


What the spec allows (where this comes from)
Item vs. transaction level

RewardTargetLineNumber = 0 → “applied to the entire transaction as a POS-defined loyalty tender.” (Tender shows up as TenderCode="loyaltyOffer"). Transaction-level only supports amountOff. (Spec section “GetRewardsResponse / AddReward” and “TenderInfo”.) 
loyalty2


Per-item methods

RewardDiscountMethod supports amountOff, percentOff, or newPrice for non-fuel and postpay fuel item lines. (Per-item, not total.) 
loyalty2


Capping to “2-for/3-for” quantities

RewardLimit type="quantity" lets you cap how many units the discount applies to (e.g., only 2 or 3). 
loyalty2


Mix & match

POS tracks promotions per line; the spec has a PromotionReason value mixAndMatchOffer for reporting, but you still apply discounts by targeting the specific line(s). If multiple lines participate, you send multiple AddReward entries (one per target line). 
loyalty2


Practical templates you can drop into your server
1) “2 for $3” (same SKU, line has SalesQuantity>=2) – per item

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>DEMO-2FOR3</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>{LINE_NUMBER}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.50</RewardValue>            <!-- $3 / 2 -->
  <RewardLimit type="quantity">2</RewardLimit>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>2FOR3</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>2 for $3</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Effect: POS prices up to 2 units on that line at $1.50 each. Extra units price normally. (Per-item, not off the whole total.) 
loyalty2


2) “Buy 3, save $1 each” – per item

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>DEMO-3X-1OFF</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>{LINE_NUMBER}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.00</RewardValue>
  <RewardLimit type="quantity">3</RewardLimit>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>$1OFFx3</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>$1 off each (up to 3)</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Effect: up to 3 units on that line are reduced by $1 each. 
loyalty2


3) “3 for $5” (average price) – per item
If the regular unit price varies, pick the per-unit target:

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>DEMO-3FOR5</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>{LINE_NUMBER}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.6667</RewardValue>         <!-- $5 / 3 -->
  <RewardLimit type="quantity">3</RewardLimit>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>3FOR5</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>3 for $5</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Effect: first 3 units on that line priced at $1.6667 each; rest at normal price. 
loyalty2


4) Transaction-level: “$2 off basket” (your current approach)

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>DEMO-BASKET-2</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>   <!-- TOTAL -->
  <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>2.00</RewardValue>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>$2OFF</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>$2 Off Basket</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Effect: reduces order total as a loyalty tender; this is not per item. 
loyalty2


Implementation notes for your server
You already print and parse all XML—great. For multi-unit deals, you’ll need the line’s LineNumber and SalesQuantity from the POS (they’re in TransactionData/TransactionLine/ItemLine). Decide at GetRewardsRequest time whether the cart meets the required quantity and then return one or more AddRewards accordingly. 
loyalty2


If the POS rings the same SKU on multiple lines (not consolidated), send one AddReward per participating line (each with its own RewardTargetLineNumber) until the total discounted quantity reaches your threshold. 
loyalty2


If you ever need a true cross-SKU “mix & match”, compute eligibility on your side using the POS-sent TransactionData, then distribute the discount across the chosen lines via multiple AddRewards (there isn’t a single “group discount” object—discounts attach to lines or to the basket). The POS will record these as promotions with reason loyaltyOffer (and conceptually aligns with the spec’s mixAndMatchOffer category). 
loyalty2

look up the target line(s) that qualify,

emit newPrice/amountOff with a RewardLimit,

or fall back to your current basket $1.00 when nothing qualifies.

You said:
yes there will be item groups where we add upcs too, and put a promotion onto that item group, so if the item exists in the item group and there is a promotion it will apply it, what are your thoughts on this method?
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 23s
Using “item groups” (a.k.a. product sets) is a solid way to drive promos. It keeps your rules simple, fast to evaluate, and easy to maintain—especially when the same offer should apply across many UPCs (sizes/flavors/brands). Here’s how I’d structure it and what to watch out for.

TL;DR – My take
✅ Good approach: group → promo rule → apply to any matching line(s).

✅ Works cleanly with Passport’s per-line rewards (RewardTargetLineNumber != 0) and with quantity caps via RewardLimit.

⚠️ Decide stacking & priority upfront (what happens if an item is in 2 groups, or a basket offer and an item offer both apply).

⚠️ Always apply per-item (line-level) for “2-for / 3-for / mix-and-match” to avoid rounding & tax headaches. Reserve transaction-level (line 0) for simple “$X off basket” or “Spend $Y get $X off”.

Data model (simple & practical)
Define two layers:

1) Item groups

group_id (string)

name

upcs: set[str] (all UPCs that qualify)

Optional: store_scope, department, brand, etc. for bulk management

2) Promotions

promo_id, name, group_id

type: one of

n_for_price (e.g., 2 for $3 → per-unit target = $1.50)

amount_off_each (e.g., buy 3, get $1 off each)

percent_off_each (e.g., 20% off, up to N units)

buy_x_get_y_free (convert to per-unit newPrice=0.00 for Y units)

Parameters:

n_required (e.g., 2 or 3)

bundle_price (for n_for_price)

amount_off, percent_off

limit_qty (cap discounted units per txn)

Windows & scope:

start_ts, end_ts, days_of_week, time_of_day (HH:MM-HH:MM)

store_ids/pricezone

Receipt text:

short, long

Conflict handling:

priority (higher wins), exclusive (if true, block lower-priority promos on same qty)

Evaluation algorithm (at GetRewardsRequest)
Parse TransactionData/TransactionLine/ItemLine to get (line_number, upc, qty, unit_price).

For each active promo:

Collect all lines whose upc ∈ group.upcs.

Sum available quantity across those lines.

If total_qty >= n_required (or meets rule), compute the number of discounted units (respect limit_qty).

Distribute the discounted units across the qualifying lines in scan order until you exhaust the discounted count.

Emit one AddReward per participating line with:

RewardTargetLineNumber = that line number

RewardDiscountMethod = newPrice, amountOff, or percentOff

RewardValue per unit (for newPrice use the per-unit target; for amountOff/percentOff it’s per unit)

RewardLimit type="quantity" = how many units on that line get the deal

Set your ReceiptDescShort/Long

If nothing qualified, optionally fall back to a basket offer (line 0).

Why per-item (line-level) is best for “2-for / 3-for”
Correct taxes: item discounts adjust taxable base the same way the POS expects.

Clear receipts: the savings appear on the affected items, not as a lump sum tender.

“Mix & match”: you can apply across lines (e.g., Coke + Sprite) without tricky basket math—just distribute discounted qty line-by-line.

Stacking & priority (important!)
Define one deterministic rule:

Example: “Highest priority wins for each unit. No stacking on the same unit. Basket-level discounts run after item-level unless marked exclusive.”

If an item is in two groups with active promos, pick the higher priority promo for that item’s discounted units.

Edge cases to handle
Multiple rings of the same UPC on different lines (don’t assume POS consolidates; distribute across lines).

Partial eligibility (e.g., 5 units with a 3-for deal → discount only 3 units).

Returns/voids/retry: idempotent behavior via your LoyaltyRewardID and the same POSSequenceID/LoyaltySequenceID.

Time windows & store scope (ignore promos out of scope).

Drop-in code sketch for your server
Here’s a compact pattern that fits into your build_get_rewards_response flow. It assumes you read TransactionData from the incoming XML and then build AddRewards. (You can adapt names to your style.)

# --- Somewhere global (config) ---
ITEM_GROUPS = {
    "CSD_20OZ": {"0004900001234", "0004900005678", "0004900009012"},  # example UPCs
    # ...
}

PROMOS = [
    {
        "promo_id": "P-2FOR3-CSD20",
        "name": "2 for $3 on CSD 20oz",
        "group": "CSD_20OZ",
        "type": "n_for_price",
        "n_required": 2,
        "bundle_price": 3.00,
        "limit_qty": 2,              # at most 2 units per txn
        "priority": 100,
        "exclusive": True,
        "short": "2FOR3",
        "long":  "2 for $3",
        # optional: time windows / store scope
    },
    {
        "promo_id": "P-3X-1OFF-CSD20",
        "name": "$1 off each on 20oz (up to 3)",
        "group": "CSD_20OZ",
        "type": "amount_off_each",
        "amount_off": 1.00,
        "n_required": 1,             # any qty qualifies, we just cap limit_qty
        "limit_qty": 3,
        "priority": 50,
        "exclusive": False,
        "short": "$1OFFx3",
        "long":  "$1 off each (up to 3)"
    },
]

def is_upc_in_group(upc):
    for gname, upcs in ITEM_GROUPS.items():
        if upc in upcs:
            yield gname

def active_promos_for_group(group_name):
    # TODO add time/store filters
    return [p for p in PROMOS if p["group"] == group_name]

def per_unit_new_price(n_for_price, n_required):
    return round(n_for_price / n_required, 4)

def lines_from_xml(root):
    # returns list of dicts: {line_no, upc, qty, unit_price}
    lines = []
    for x in root.findall(".//TransactionLine/ItemLine"):
        ln = int(x.findtext("LineNumber", "0"))
        upc = (x.findtext("POSCode") or "").strip()
        qty = float(x.findtext("SalesQuantity", "1"))
        price = float(x.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        lines.append({"line_no": ln, "upc": upc, "qty": qty, "unit_price": price})
    return lines

def build_rewards_for_item_groups(root):
    # 1) Build map group -> [lines]
    lines = lines_from_xml(root)
    group_to_lines = {}
    for ln in lines:
        for gname in is_upc_in_group(ln["upc"]):
            group_to_lines.setdefault(gname, []).append(ln)

    add_rewards = []

    # 2) For each group, evaluate each promo (highest priority first)
    for gname, glines in group_to_lines.items():
        promos = sorted(active_promos_for_group(gname), key=lambda p: -p["priority"])
        # Track per-unit assignment to avoid stacking the same unit twice
        remaining_units_per_line = {ln["line_no"]: ln["qty"] for ln in glines}

        for promo in promos:
            # Compute total eligible units still free for this promo
            total_free = int(sum(remaining_units_per_line[ln["line_no"]] for ln in glines))
            if total_free <= 0:
                continue

            # Check threshold
            if total_free < int(promo.get("n_required", 1)):
                continue

            # Determine discounted unit count (respect limit_qty)
            discounted_units = total_free
            if "limit_qty" in promo and promo["limit_qty"] is not None:
                discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(promo["limit_qty"]))

            if discounted_units <= 0:
                continue

            # Figure per-unit reward
            rmethod, rvalue = None, None
            if promo["type"] == "n_for_price":
                rmethod = "newPrice"
                rvalue = per_unit_new_price(promo["bundle_price"], promo["n_required"])
            elif promo["type"] == "amount_off_each":
                rmethod = "amountOff"
                rvalue = promo["amount_off"]
            elif promo["type"] == "percent_off_each":
                rmethod = "percentOff"
                rvalue = promo["percent_off"]
            elif promo["type"] == "buy_x_get_y_free":
                rmethod = "newPrice"
                rvalue = 0.00
                discounted_units = promo["y_free"]
            else:
                continue  # unknown type

            # Distribute discounted units across lines (scan order)
            for ln in glines:
                if discounted_units <= 0:
                    break
                take = int(min(remaining_units_per_line[ln["line_no"]], discounted_units))
                if take <= 0:
                    continue

                add_rewards.append({
                    "line_no": ln["line_no"],
                    "loyalty_reward_id": promo["promo_id"],
                    "method": rmethod,
                    "value": rvalue,
                    "limit_qty": take,
                    "short": promo["short"],
                    "long": promo["long"],
                })
                remaining_units_per_line[ln["line_no"]] -= take
                discounted_units -= take

            # If exclusive, don’t allow lower-priority promos to touch units already discounted
            if promo.get("exclusive", False):
                # Units are already consumed by this promo via remaining_units_per_line
                pass

    return add_rewards
Now in your build_get_rewards_response, instead of always returning your $1 off basket, you can:

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

    rewards = build_rewards_for_item_groups(root)

    # Fallback: if no item-level rewards, keep your $1 basket offer (optional)
    if not rewards:
        rewards = [{
            "line_no": 0,
            "loyalty_reward_id": "DEMO-1OFF",
            "method": "amountOff",
            "value": DISCOUNT_VALUE,
            "limit_qty": None,       # not used for line 0
            "short": RECEIPT_SHORT,
            "long": RECEIPT_LONG,
        }]

    # Build XML
    add_xmls = []
    for r in rewards:
        limit_xml = f'<RewardLimit type="quantity">{r["limit_qty"]}</RewardLimit>' if r["limit_qty"] else ""
        add_xmls.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{r["loyalty_reward_id"]}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{r["line_no"]}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>{r["method"]}</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{r["value"]:.4f}</RewardValue>
      {limit_xml}
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{r["short"]}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{r["long"]}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    {''.join(add_xmls)}
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
Operational tips
Performance: keep ITEM_GROUPS in memory as sets; reload on a timer or when you receive a webhook/signal.

Auditing: log promo decisions (group match, quantities, chosen rule).

Receipts: keep short ≤ 8 chars, long ≤ 24 chars (your code already follows this pattern).

Taxes: per-item discounts typically auto-adjust taxable amount correctly; basket-level tender may behave differently—check your store tax settings.

Fuel vs non-fuel: use line-level only for non-fuel and postpay fuel (as per spec constraints); prepay fuel is different.

If you want, I can tailor the rule engine to your real UPC lists and the exact promos you plan to run (2-for-$X, 3-for-$Y, mix-and-match between brands, etc.) and wire it into your current server.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 16s
Great—those two cover most real promos. Here’s a clean, reliable way to do both with your “item group” approach, wired into your existing server.

How to model them
A) “N for $PRICE” (n_for_price)
When it triggers: total eligible qty across the group ≥ n_required.

How much to discount: set per-unit target price = bundle_price / n_required (use RewardDiscountMethod = newPrice).

How many units get the deal:
discounted_units = floor(total_qty / n_required) * n_required
(Optionally cap with limit_qty.)

Where to apply: distribute those discounted_units across the qualifying lines (scan order, or by price—your choice).

Why per-item: taxes and receipt lines stay correct.

B) “Buy X get Y free” (buy_x_get_y_free)
When it triggers: total eligible qty across the group ≥ x + y.

How many units are free:
free_units = floor(total_qty / (x + y)) * y
(Optionally cap with limit_qty.)

Which units are free: most retailers comp the cheapest units first (to avoid over-discounting). If you want simpler, do scan order—just be consistent.

How to apply: set RewardDiscountMethod = newPrice and RewardValue = 0.00 for the free units, distributing free_units across the lines (again, cheapest-first or scan order).

Drop-in code you can paste (fits your current server)
Add these helpers (they work on your parsed TransactionData lines):

def collect_group_lines(root, upc_set):
    """Return list of eligible lines: [{line_no, upc, qty, unit_price}]"""
    lines = []
    for x in root.findall(".//TransactionLine/ItemLine"):
        ln = int(x.findtext("LineNumber", "0"))
        upc = (x.findtext("POSCode") or "").strip()
        if upc not in upc_set:
            continue
        qty = float(x.findtext("SalesQuantity", "1"))
        price = float(x.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        if qty <= 0:
            continue
        lines.append({"line_no": ln, "upc": upc, "qty": qty, "unit_price": price})
    # You can choose order policy here:
    # - scan order (as is), or
    # - cheapest-first: lines.sort(key=lambda d: d["unit_price"])
    return lines

def emit_line_reward(line_no, promo_id, method, value, take_qty, short, long):
    return f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{promo_id}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{line_no}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>{method}</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{value:.4f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardLimit type="quantity">{int(take_qty)}</RewardLimit>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{short}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{long}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip()

def n_for_price_rewards(root, upc_set, promo_id, n_required, bundle_price,
                        limit_qty=None, short="N4$", long_desc="N for $"):
    lines = collect_group_lines(root, upc_set)
    if not lines:
        return []
    total_qty = int(sum(int(l["qty"]) for l in lines))
    if total_qty < n_required:
        return []
    # How many units qualify
    discounted_units = (total_qty // n_required) * n_required
    if limit_qty is not None:
        discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(limit_qty))
    if discounted_units <= 0:
        return []
    # Per-unit target price
    per_unit_price = round(bundle_price / n_required, 4)

    rewards_xml = []
    remaining = discounted_units
    # Choose your policy: scan order or cheapest-first (sort by price)
    # lines.sort(key=lambda d: d["unit_price"])  # uncomment for cheapest-first
    for l in lines:
        if remaining <= 0:
            break
        take = int(min(int(l["qty"]), remaining))
        if take <= 0:
            continue
        rewards_xml.append(
            emit_line_reward(l["line_no"], promo_id, "newPrice", per_unit_price, take, short, long_desc)
        )
        remaining -= take
    return rewards_xml

def buy_x_get_y_free_rewards(root, upc_set, promo_id, x_required, y_free,
                             limit_qty=None, short="BOGO", long_desc="Buy X Get Y Free",
                             cheapest_first=True):
    lines = collect_group_lines(root, upc_set)
    if not lines:
        return []
    total_qty = int(sum(int(l["qty"]) for l in lines))
    group_size = x_required + y_free
    if total_qty < group_size:
        return []
    free_units = (total_qty // group_size) * y_free
    if limit_qty is not None:
        free_units = min(free_units, int(limit_qty))
    if free_units <= 0:
        return []

    rewards_xml = []
    remaining_free = free_units

    # Choose which units become free: cheapest-first is typical
    if cheapest_first:
        lines = sorted(lines, key=lambda d: d["unit_price"])
    # else keep scan order

    for l in lines:
        if remaining_free <= 0:
            break
        take = int(min(int(l["qty"]), remaining_free))
        if take <= 0:
            continue
        rewards_xml.append(
            emit_line_reward(l["line_no"], promo_id, "newPrice", 0.00, take, short, long_desc)
        )
        remaining_free -= take
    return rewards_xml
Then, inside your build_get_rewards_response, call the helpers for each active promo on the item group, concatenate their <AddReward> chunks, and return them. Example:

# Example config in memory (replace with your real UPC sets)
ITEM_GROUPS = {
    "CSD_20OZ": {"0004900001234", "0004900005678", "0004900009012"},
    "CHIPS_2OZ": {"0002840012345", "0002840098765"},
}

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

    add_xmls = []

    # ---- N FOR PRICE example: 2 for $3 on 20oz CSD (cap 4 units/txn) ----
    add_xmls += n_for_price_rewards(
        root,
        upc_set=ITEM_GROUPS["CSD_20OZ"],
        promo_id="P-2FOR3-CSD20",
        n_required=2,
        bundle_price=3.00,
        limit_qty=4,
        short="2FOR3",
        long_desc="2 for $3"
    )

    # ---- BUY X GET Y FREE example: Buy 2 Get 1 free on chips (cap 3 free/txn) ----
    add_xmls += buy_x_get_y_free_rewards(
        root,
        upc_set=ITEM_GROUPS["CHIPS_2OZ"],
        promo_id="P-B2G1-CHIPS",
        x_required=2,
        y_free=1,
        limit_qty=3,
        short="B2G1",
        long_desc="Buy 2 Get 1 Free",
        cheapest_first=True
    )

    # Optional: if no item-level rewards fired, fall back to your $1 basket offer
    if not add_xmls:
        add_xmls.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    {''.join(add_xmls)}
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
Policy choices (pick now, avoid surprises later)
Ordering rule: for BxGy, decide “cheapest units are free” vs “scan order.” “Cheapest-free” is common and protects margin.

Caps: always set sensible limit_qty (e.g., “2 bundles per txn”).

Conflicts: if an item is eligible for both “N for $X” and “B2G1,” choose one per unit (highest priority), not both.

Rounding: use 4 decimal places internally; POS will format to currency on receipt.

Partial bundles: if someone buys 3 under “2 for $3,” only 2 get newPrice=$1.50; the extra rings at normal price.

Multiple lines of same UPC: distribute discounted units across lines until you exhaust the eligible count.

If you share your actual UPC sets and promo caps, I can plug them straight into the helpers above and hand you a ready-to-run build_get_rewards_response.

You said:
so will we do line item discounts for the 2 for promo or off entire purchase discount
ChatGPT said:
Line-item discounts.

For “2-for-$X” (or “3-for-$Y”), you should set per-item prices with RewardTargetLineNumber = <that line> and RewardDiscountMethod = newPrice (value = bundle_price / N). That keeps taxes right, shows savings on the affected items, handles partial bundles cleanly (only N units discounted, extras at normal price), and plays nicely with mix-and-match across multiple lines.

Use transaction-level (RewardTargetLineNumber = 0, amountOff) only for true basket deals (e.g., “$2 off your total,” “Spend $15, get $3 off”). Those aren’t ideal for N-for pricing because they lump the discount into a tender, can confuse tax treatment, and make receipts/audits murkier.

Quick template for 2-for-$3 on a line:

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>P-2FOR3</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>{LINE_NUMBER}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.50</RewardValue>     <!-- $3 / 2 -->
  <RewardLimit type="quantity">2</RewardLimit>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>2FOR3</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>2 for $3</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Bottom line: 2-for promos = line-level; basket coupons/threshold offers = transaction-level.

ok can you write the entire code that builds off this one:

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server
-------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• $1.00 OFF any transaction when ANY LoyaltyID (e.g., phone #) is entered
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"   # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000             # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Hard-coded reward: $1.00 off transaction total (line 0)
DISCOUNT_VALUE = 1.00
REWARD_ID      = "DEMO-1OFF"
RECEIPT_SHORT  = "$1OFF"            # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG   = "Loyalty $1 Off"   # <= 24 chars

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""
    # Transaction-level reward (line 0), amountOff = $1.00, instant apply.
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>LOYALTY $1 OFF APPLIED</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>$1 Loyalty discount available</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply $1 off
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()

and make it 2 for 1.00 if it recognizes two of the same upc numbers? this should be a complete code similar to the one i just provided
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 17s
Absolutely — here’s a drop-in, full script that keeps your same server structure but switches the reward logic to a “2 for $1.00” item-level promo for any UPC where the cart contains at least two of the same UPC (even if they’re split across multiple lines). It prices the eligible pairs at $0.50 each using RewardDiscountMethod=newPrice, and distributes the discounted units across lines for that UPC. If nothing qualifies, it returns no rewards (you can re-enable the basket $1 fallback by uncommenting the noted block).

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server – 2-for-$1.00 Item Promo
--------------------------------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• Applies a "2 for $1.00" promo per-UPC (line-level newPrice=$0.50) when any LoyaltyID is entered
  - Pairs are counted per UPC across the whole cart (even if split across lines)
  - Only complete pairs are discounted (e.g., qty 3 → 2 discounted; 1 remains normal)
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"     # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000               # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# --- 2-for-$1.00 promo config (per UPC) ---
N_REQUIRED         = 2
BUNDLE_PRICE       = 1.00
PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE = round(BUNDLE_PRICE / N_REQUIRED, 4)   # $0.50
PROMO_ID           = "DEMO-2FOR1"                          # appears as LoyaltyRewardID
RECEIPT_SHORT      = "2FOR1"                               # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG       = "2 for $1"                            # <= 24 chars
# Optional cap per UPC per txn (None means unlimited bundles per UPC)
LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC = None  # e.g., set to 4 to cap at two bundles per UPC

# --- (Optional) Basket fallback (disabled by default) ---
# DISCOUNT_VALUE    = 1.00
# BASKET_REWARD_ID  = "DEMO-1OFF"
# BASKET_SHORT      = "$1OFF"
# BASKET_LONG       = "Loyalty $1 Off"

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Helpers for 2-for-$1.00
# =========================
def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Returns list of dicts: {line_no:int, upc:str, qty:int, unit_price:float}
    Quantities are coerced to int bundles (floor), since promo applies to whole units.
    """
    lines = []
    for x in root.findall(".//TransactionLine/ItemLine"):
        try:
            ln   = int(x.findtext("LineNumber", "0"))
        except Exception:
            ln = 0
        upc  = (x.findtext("POSCode") or "").strip()
        try:
            qty  = int(float(x.findtext("SalesQuantity", "1")))
        except Exception:
            qty = 1
        try:
            price = float(x.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        except Exception:
            price = 0.0
        if upc and qty > 0:
            lines.append({"line_no": ln, "upc": upc, "qty": qty, "unit_price": price})
    return lines

def build_2for1_rewards_xml(root: ET.Element):
    """
    For each UPC, if total qty >= 2, discount pairs to $0.50 each (newPrice).
    Distribute discounted units across lines with that UPC.
    Returns list of <AddReward> XML strings.
    """
    lines = parse_item_lines(root)
    if not lines:
        return []

    # Group by UPC
    upc_to_lines = {}
    for l in lines:
        upc_to_lines.setdefault(l["upc"], []).append(l)

    add_rewards = []

    for upc, lnlist in upc_to_lines.items():
        total_qty = sum(l["qty"] for l in lnlist)
        if total_qty < N_REQUIRED:
            continue

        # Eligible discounted units = complete pairs only
        discounted_units = (total_qty // N_REQUIRED) * N_REQUIRED

        # Optional per-UPC cap
        if LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC is not None:
            discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC))

        if discounted_units <= 0:
            continue

        # Distribute across lines (scan order). If you prefer cheapest-first, uncomment next line:
        # lnlist = sorted(lnlist, key=lambda d: d["unit_price"])

        remaining = discounted_units
        for l in lnlist:
            if remaining <= 0:
                break
            take = min(l["qty"], remaining)
            if take <= 0:
                continue

            add_rewards.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{PROMO_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{l["line_no"]}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE:.4f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardLimit type="quantity">{int(take)}</RewardLimit>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

            remaining -= take

    return add_rewards

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

    # Build 2-for-$1.00 rewards
    add_xmls = build_2for1_rewards_xml(root)

    # Optional: fallback $1 off basket if nothing qualified (disabled by default)
    # if not add_xmls:
    #     add_xmls.append(f"""
    # <AddReward>
    #   <LoyaltyRewardID>{BASKET_REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
    #   <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
    #   <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
    #   <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
    #   <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
    #   <RewardReceiptDescShort>{BASKET_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
    #   <RewardReceiptDescLong>{BASKET_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    # </AddReward>""".rstrip())

    # If still no rewards, just return a valid response with no RewardActions to avoid POS errors
    rewards_block = f"<RewardActions>\n    {''.join(add_xmls)}\n  </RewardActions>" if add_xmls else "<RewardActions/>"

    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  {rewards_block}
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>2 FOR $1 APPLIED WHERE ELIGIBLE</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>2 for $1.00 promo active</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply 2-for-$1 rewards where eligible
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()
Notes
Per-item (line-level) only: The discount is applied with newPrice on each affected line, keeping taxes/receipts correct.

Pairs only: For qty=3, 2 units get $0.50; the extra rings normally.

Split lines: If the same UPC is rung on multiple lines, it still pairs correctly across those lines.

Caps: To cap per-UPC discounts (e.g., max 2 bundles → 4 discounted units), set LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC = 4.

Fallback basket coupon: If you still want your old $1 off basket when no pairs are present, uncomment the “basket fallback” block.

You said:
still uses any phone number right?
ChatGPT said:
Yep! With the script I gave you, any LoyaltyID (e.g., any phone number) is accepted:

GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse → PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes" (POS will ask for loyalty).

GetRewardsResponse → always returns <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">…</LoyaltyIDValidFlag> regardless of the value, so any phone number (or any string) works.

If you want to restrict it later, drop in a validator and flip that flag:

import re

def is_valid_phone(loy_id: str) -> bool:
    # Example: 10–11 digits, ignore punctuation/spaces
    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", loy_id or "")
    return digits.isdigit() and 10 <= len(digits) <= 11

# In build_get_rewards_response:
loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
valid = is_valid_phone(loyalty_id)
masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

if not valid:
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions/>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
You can also reject at the “are we online / should I even prompt?” step by returning <PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="no"/> if you want to disable entry entirely for certain stores/hours.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 22s
Yep — I see why your promo didn’t fire.

In both GetRewardsRequests, only one eligible item is actually in the live cart:

Line 1 (Cup of Ice, PLU 16794) → status="void" → ignore

Line 3 (KIND bar, UPC 602652170300) → status="void" and discountable="no" → ignore

Line 4 (Cup of Ice, PLU 16794) → status="normal", discountable="yes" → counts (qty=1)

Line 5 (Celsius, UPC 889392021394) → status="normal", discountable="yes" → different UPC

So for UPC/PLU 16794 you only had qty = 1 in “normal” lines. My script (as sent) counted everything, but you want it to ignore voided lines and non-discountable lines — otherwise N-for logic won’t match what the cashier really has on the check.

Quick fix (drop-in)
Update your line parser so it only counts item lines that are status="normal" and discountable="yes":

def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Returns list of dicts: {line_no:int, upc:str, qty:int, unit_price:float}
    Counts ONLY non-void, discountable item lines.
    """
    lines = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
            continue  # ignore void/return/etc.

        x = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if x is None:
            continue
        if (x.get("discountable") or "").strip().lower() != "yes":
            continue  # respect POS "discountable" flag

        ln_txt = tline.findtext("./LineNumber", "0")
        try:
            ln = int(ln_txt)
        except Exception:
            ln = 0

        upc = (x.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        if not upc:
            continue

        # Prefer SalesQuantity; fall back to SellingUnits
        qty_txt = x.findtext("SalesQuantity", x.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
        try:
            qty = int(float(qty_txt))
        except Exception:
            qty = 1

        price_txt = x.findtext("UnitPrice", x.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", "0"))
        try:
            price = float(price_txt)
        except Exception:
            price = 0.0

        if qty > 0:
            lines.append({"line_no": ln, "upc": upc, "qty": qty, "unit_price": price})
    return lines
Optionally add a little debug so you can see what qualified:

def build_2for1_rewards_xml(root: ET.Element):
    lines = parse_item_lines(root)
    log(f"Eligible lines: {lines}")  # DEBUG: shows only counted lines
    ...

You said:
will it overwrite the existing discount? how does that work if there is already a discount at the pos
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 32s
Great question. Short answer: Passport will not “overwrite” a store-programmed discount on its own; your loyalty response simply adds another discount layer—if the line is discountable. Whether that stacks or you effectively do a “best price wins” is up to how you build the reward you send.

Here’s how to think about it and a safe way to implement it:

What typically happens
If the item is flagged discountable="yes", Passport will accept your <AddReward> and apply it in addition to any existing POS markdown/sale—unless your server logic avoids it.

If discountable="no", Passport won’t let you touch that line (so no overwrite/stack).

Using RewardDiscountMethod="newPrice" acts like a price floor: it sets the final per-unit price to your value (e.g., $0.50) if and only if that would reduce the price. It won’t raise prices.

Choose your policy (recommended)
For multipacks like “2 for $1” the clean policy is:

Best-price floor
If a unit already has a deal that makes it ≤ $0.50, do nothing.
If it’s > $0.50, send newPrice = 0.50 for just the qualifying units.

That way you never “double dip” below your target, and you don’t fight with store promos. You’re effectively guaranteeing “no worse than 2 for $1” without stacking past that floor.

Tiny patch to the code I gave you
Drop this helper in and call it where we emit rewards. It checks the current per-unit price on each line and only sends an override if it’s needed.

def current_unit_price(line_dict):
    # Prefer UnitPrice if present; otherwise ActualSalesPrice; fallback to RegularSellPrice
    price = line_dict.get("unit_price", 0.0)
    if not price or price <= 0:
        price = line_dict.get("actual_price", 0.0)
    if not price or price <= 0:
        price = line_dict.get("regular_price", 0.0)
    return float(price or 0.0)
Update your line parser to capture those fields (so we can compare against your $0.50 target):

def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    lines = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
            continue
        x = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if x is None:
            continue
        if (x.get("discountable") or "").strip().lower() != "yes":
            continue

        ln = int(tline.findtext("./LineNumber", "0") or 0)
        upc = (x.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        if not upc:
            continue

        qty = int(float(x.findtext("SalesQuantity", x.findtext("SellingUnits", "1")) or 1))
        unit_price = float(x.findtext("UnitPrice", "0") or 0)
        actual_price = float(x.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", "0") or 0)
        regular_price = float(x.findtext("RegularSellPrice", "0") or 0)

        if qty > 0:
            lines.append({
                "line_no": ln,
                "upc": upc,
                "qty": qty,
                "unit_price": unit_price,
                "actual_price": actual_price,
                "regular_price": regular_price,
            })
    return lines
And in build_2for1_rewards_xml, only emit newPrice for units whose current price is above your floor:

def build_2for1_rewards_xml(root: ET.Element):
    lines = parse_item_lines(root)
    if not lines:
        return []

    # Group by UPC
    upc_to_lines = {}
    for l in lines:
        upc_to_lines.setdefault(l["upc"], []).append(l)

    add_rewards = []

    for upc, lnlist in upc_to_lines.items():
        total_qty = sum(l["qty"] for l in lnlist)
        if total_qty < N_REQUIRED:
            continue

        # Eligible pairs
        discounted_units = (total_qty // N_REQUIRED) * N_REQUIRED
        if LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC is not None:
            discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC))
        if discounted_units <= 0:
            continue

        # Distribute across lines; only discount units that are priced above the floor
        remaining = discounted_units
        for l in lnlist:
            if remaining <= 0:
                break

            floor_price = PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE
            unit_now = current_unit_price(l)

            if unit_now <= 0 or unit_now <= floor_price:
                # This line is already at/under the 2-for price; skip it (no stack below floor)
                continue

            take = min(l["qty"], remaining)
            if take <= 0:
                continue

            add_rewards.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{PROMO_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{l["line_no"]}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{floor_price:.4f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardLimit type="quantity">{int(take)}</RewardLimit>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

            remaining -= take

    return add_rewards
TL;DR
It won’t “overwrite” store discounts; loyalty is another layer.

By sending newPrice only when current price > $0.50, you avoid stacking below your intended floor and you respect existing sales/markdowns.

If you ever want true non-stacking (i.e., skip any item that already has any discount), just add a check like:

if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
    continue  # item already on sale; skip loyalty
Want me to roll these changes into your full script and paste the complete file again?

You said:
so with this code if we do a celsius it will give two celcius for 1.00?
ChatGPT said:
Yes—if you ring two of the same Celsius UPC (e.g., 889392021394) on live (status="normal") lines and they’re discountable="yes", the code will apply the 2-for-$1 deal by setting each of those two units to $0.50 (newPrice at the line level).

Quick checklist to make it fire on Celsius:

Same exact UPC scanned twice (or qty=2 on one line)

Lines must be status="normal" (not void)

discountable="yes" on those lines

Unit price currently > $0.50 (the “best-price floor” logic won’t lower an item already ≤ $0.50)

Notes:

Split across multiple lines is fine—the script pools quantities per UPC and then distributes the discount back across those lines.

Different Celsius flavors with different UPCs won’t mix & match under this script. If you want “any Celsius flavor 2 for $1,” we can flip it to use an item group of Celsius UPCs.

You said:
even though celsius already has a discount in place?
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 21s
Short answer: it depends on which version of the code you’re running.

If you ran the first “full script” I sent (no floor check):
Yes, it will still apply the 2-for-$1 to Celsius even if Celsius already has a POS discount. It will force the qualifying units to $0.50 using newPrice, which could stack with the store promo and (in a weird case) even raise the unit price if the store promo already made it < $0.50. Not ideal.

If you added the “best-price floor” patch I gave you:
It will only send newPrice=$0.50 when the current unit price is above $0.50.

If Celsius is already discounted to ≤ $0.50, your server sends no reward for those units (so it never raises price or double-discounts below your floor).

If you also added the optional “skip if already on sale” check (actual_price < regular_price), it will not stack on any item that’s already discounted at the POS.

What I recommend (to be safe)
Use the best-price floor logic so you never raise a price and you only lower it to your target when needed. If you want no stacking at all with store sales, also include the “skip if already on sale” check.

If you want, I can paste your full file with those safeguards baked in so you can just replace and run.
