ChatGPT said:

Thought for 55s
No — in that last transaction the Celsius did not become 2-for-$1. Your server returned <RewardActions/> (empty), so no loyalty price change was sent. The two Celsius cans still show a store promo on each line:

UPC 889392021417 on lines 1 and 4 are discountable="yes" but each has a <Promotion … PromotionReason="mixAndMatchOffer"> with amounts 0.45 and 0.44. The POS subtotal for the pair is $5.49 (taxable), i.e., about $2.75 each—not $0.50 each.

Why your code didn’t fire (likely one of these):

You added the “don’t stack if already on sale” safeguard (skip when ActualSalesPrice < RegularSellPrice). In your payload, RegularSellPrice=3.19 and ActualSalesPrice=0 (how Passport represents a promo’d line), so the “already on sale” check would skip loyalty.

Or you kept the best-price floor and also considered lines already ≤ $0.50 as “no change.” Here they’re ~2.75 each, so this wouldn’t block—so #1 is the prime suspect.

If you want the loyalty to override store promos down to $0.50 each (i.e., stack or “best price wins”), do either of the following:

Option A — Allow stacking (override to $0.50 even if on sale)
Remove or comment out the “skip if already on sale” check:

# if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
#     continue  # REMOVE this to allow stacking
Keep the best-price floor (only send newPrice=0.50 if current > 0.50). This will push those two Celsius lines to $0.50 each despite the store mix-and-match.

Option B — Keep no-stacking, but log why it skipped (debug)
Add a quick debug so you can see the decision:

log(f"Line {l['line_no']} {l['upc']} qty={l['qty']} unit_now={unit_now} regular={l['regular_price']} actual={l['actual_price']}")
if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
    log(f"Skipping line {l['line_no']} (already on sale at POS)")
    continue
Quick sanity test
Scan two of the same Celsius UPC again.

If you allow stacking, your next GetRewardsResponse should include two <AddReward> entries with RewardDiscountMethod=newPrice and RewardValue=0.5000 (split across the lines), and the POS total should drop to $1.00 pre-tax for that pair.

If you keep no-stacking, you’ll continue to see <RewardActions/> when the POS promo is present.

Want me to paste your full file with “stacking allowed” baked in and a debug line so you can verify it live?

You said:
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server – 2-for-$1.00 Item Promo
--------------------------------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• Applies a "2 for $1.00" promo per-UPC (line-level newPrice=$0.50) when any LoyaltyID is entered
  - Pairs are counted per UPC across the whole cart (even if split across lines)
  - Only complete pairs are discounted (e.g., qty 3 → 2 discounted; 1 remains normal)
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"     # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000               # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# --- 2-for-$1.00 promo config (per UPC) ---
N_REQUIRED         = 2
BUNDLE_PRICE       = 1.00
PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE = round(BUNDLE_PRICE / N_REQUIRED, 4)   # $0.50
PROMO_ID           = "DEMO-2FOR1"                          # appears as LoyaltyRewardID
RECEIPT_SHORT      = "2FOR1"                               # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG       = "2 for $1"                            # <= 24 chars
# Optional cap per UPC per txn (None means unlimited bundles per UPC)
LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC = None  # e.g., set to 4 to cap at two bundles per UPC

# --- (Optional) Basket fallback (disabled by default) ---
# DISCOUNT_VALUE    = 1.00
# BASKET_REWARD_ID  = "DEMO-1OFF"
# BASKET_SHORT      = "$1OFF"
# BASKET_LONG       = "Loyalty $1 Off"

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Helpers for 2-for-$1.00
# =========================
def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Returns list of dicts: {line_no:int, upc:str, qty:int, unit_price:float}
    Quantities are coerced to int bundles (floor), since promo applies to whole units.
    """
    lines = []
    for x in root.findall(".//TransactionLine/ItemLine"):
        try:
            ln   = int(x.findtext("LineNumber", "0"))
        except Exception:
            ln = 0
        upc  = (x.findtext("POSCode") or "").strip()
        try:
            qty  = int(float(x.findtext("SalesQuantity", "1")))
        except Exception:
            qty = 1
        try:
            price = float(x.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        except Exception:
            price = 0.0
        if upc and qty > 0:
            lines.append({"line_no": ln, "upc": upc, "qty": qty, "unit_price": price})
    return lines

def build_2for1_rewards_xml(root: ET.Element):
    """
    For each UPC, if total qty >= 2, discount pairs to $0.50 each (newPrice).
    Distribute discounted units across lines with that UPC.
    Returns list of <AddReward> XML strings.
    """
    lines = parse_item_lines(root)
    if not lines:
        return []

    # Group by UPC
    upc_to_lines = {}
    for l in lines:
        upc_to_lines.setdefault(l["upc"], []).append(l)

    add_rewards = []

    for upc, lnlist in upc_to_lines.items():
        total_qty = sum(l["qty"] for l in lnlist)
        if total_qty < N_REQUIRED:
            continue

        # Eligible discounted units = complete pairs only
        discounted_units = (total_qty // N_REQUIRED) * N_REQUIRED

        # Optional per-UPC cap
        if LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC is not None:
            discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC))

        if discounted_units <= 0:
            continue

        # Distribute across lines (scan order). If you prefer cheapest-first, uncomment next line:
        # lnlist = sorted(lnlist, key=lambda d: d["unit_price"])

        remaining = discounted_units
        for l in lnlist:
            if remaining <= 0:
                break
            take = min(l["qty"], remaining)
            if take <= 0:
                continue

            add_rewards.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{PROMO_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{l["line_no"]}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE:.4f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardLimit type="quantity">{int(take)}</RewardLimit>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

            remaining -= take

    return add_rewards

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

    # Build 2-for-$1.00 rewards
    add_xmls = build_2for1_rewards_xml(root)

    # Optional: fallback $1 off basket if nothing qualified (disabled by default)
    # if not add_xmls:
    #     add_xmls.append(f"""
    # <AddReward>
    #   <LoyaltyRewardID>{BASKET_REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
    #   <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
    #   <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
    #   <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
    #   <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
    #   <RewardReceiptDescShort>{BASKET_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
    #   <RewardReceiptDescLong>{BASKET_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    # </AddReward>""".rstrip())

    # If still no rewards, just return a valid response with no RewardActions to avoid POS errors
    rewards_block = f"<RewardActions>\n    {''.join(add_xmls)}\n  </RewardActions>" if add_xmls else "<RewardActions/>"

    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  {rewards_block}
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>2 FOR $1 APPLIED WHERE ELIGIBLE</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>2 for $1.00 promo active</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply 2-for-$1 rewards where eligible
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()

ChatGPT said:
Got it—I see why your 2-for-$1 didn’t fire with this code.

What’s wrong in this file
Your parse_item_lines is pulling fields from the wrong XML paths and it doesn’t filter out void / non-discountable lines:

You iterate ".//TransactionLine/ItemLine" but then read LineNumber inside ItemLine. In Passport XML, LineNumber lives under TransactionLine, not ItemLine → you were sending RewardTargetLineNumber=0 (or never building rewards).

You read POSCode directly, but in real payloads it’s under ItemCode/POSCode → your UPCs came back empty, so no grouping/pairs were found.

You don’t check TransactionLine@status="normal" or ItemLine@discountable="yes" → you can accidentally count void lines or be blocked by non-discountable ones.

Minimal drop-in fix
Replace your entire parse_item_lines with this version, then keep the rest as-is:

def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Returns list of dicts: {line_no:int, upc:str, qty:int, unit_price:float,
                            actual_price:float, regular_price:float}
    Counts ONLY non-void, discountable item lines. Uses correct XML paths.
    """
    lines = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        # Only live lines
        if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
            continue

        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is None:
            continue

        # Respect POS discountable flag
        if (il.get("discountable") or "").strip().lower() != "yes":
            continue

        # Line number is on TransactionLine
        try:
            ln = int(tline.findtext("./LineNumber", "0"))
        except Exception:
            ln = 0

        # UPC/PLU path
        upc = (il.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        if not upc:
            continue

        # Quantity: prefer SalesQuantity then SellingUnits
        qty_txt = il.findtext("SalesQuantity", il.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
        try:
            qty = int(float(qty_txt))
        except Exception:
            qty = 1

        # Prices for “best price floor” decisions or debugging
        def _f(x): 
            try: return float(x)
            except: return 0.0
        unit_price   = _f(il.findtext("UnitPrice",   "0"))
        actual_price = _f(il.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", "0"))
        regular_price= _f(il.findtext("RegularSellPrice", "0"))

        if qty > 0:
            lines.append({
                "line_no": ln,
                "upc": upc,
                "qty": qty,
                "unit_price": unit_price,
                "actual_price": actual_price,
                "regular_price": regular_price,
            })
    return lines
Optionally add a one-liner in build_2for1_rewards_xml to see what’s being counted:

log(f"Eligible lines -> {lines}")
If you want the loyalty to override store promos (stacking)
Your current build applies newPrice=$0.50 to qualifying units unconditionally. That’s fine if you want loyalty to win even when a store mix-and-match is present. If you do not want stacking, add a skip like:

# inside the loop before emitting <AddReward>
if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
    # already on sale at POS; skip this line if you don't want stacking
    continue
(If you want a “best price floor”, only emit newPrice=0.50 when the current unit is > 0.50; I can drop that snippet in too.)

Why Celsius didn’t go 2-for-$1 in your log
Both Celsius lines were status="normal" and discountable="yes", but your parser wasn’t reading the UPC or the correct line numbers, so no pairs were detected and no <AddReward> was built → <RewardActions/>.

Apply the parser fix above and try again with two of the same Celsius UPC. You should then see two <AddReward> entries with:

RewardTargetLineNumber = each line’s real number (1 and 4 in your example)

RewardDiscountMethod = newPrice

RewardValue = 0.5000

RewardLimit type="quantity" for the units on that line

If you want, I can paste your full file with these fixes (plus optional stacking/no-stacking switch) baked in.

ChatGPT said:
Here’s the fully fixed script. It:

Parses line numbers and UPCs from the correct paths

Ignores void lines and non-discountable items

Applies 2-for-$1.00 per UPC as line-level newPrice=$0.50

Uses a best-price floor so it never raises price (it overrides store promos down to $0.50, but won’t go below $0.50 if a store promo is already cheaper)

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server – 2-for-$1.00 Item Promo (FIXED)
---------------------------------------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• Applies a "2 for $1.00" promo per-UPC (line-level newPrice=$0.50) when any LoyaltyID is entered
  - Pairs are counted per UPC across the whole cart (even if split across lines)
  - Only complete pairs are discounted (e.g., qty 3 → 2 discounted; 1 remains normal)
  - Best-price floor: only lowers price to $0.50 if current per-unit > $0.50 (never raises)
• Handles: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"         # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000                   # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2" # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# --- 2-for-$1.00 promo config (per UPC) ---
N_REQUIRED         = 2
BUNDLE_PRICE       = 1.00
PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE = round(BUNDLE_PRICE / N_REQUIRED, 4)   # $0.50
PROMO_ID           = "DEMO-2FOR1"                          # LoyaltyRewardID
RECEIPT_SHORT      = "2FOR1"                               # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG       = "2 for $1"                            # <= 24 chars
# Optional cap per UPC per txn (None means unlimited bundles per UPC)
LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC = None  # e.g., 4 caps at two bundles per UPC

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Helpers for 2-for-$1.00
# =========================
def _f(num_str, default=0.0):
    try:
        return float(num_str)
    except Exception:
        return default

def parse_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Returns list of dicts:
      {line_no:int, upc:str, qty:int,
       unit_price:float, actual_price:float, regular_price:float}
    Counts ONLY non-void TransactionLine with discountable="yes" ItemLine.
    Uses correct XML paths for LineNumber and POSCode.
    """
    lines = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
            continue  # ignore void/returned/etc.

        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is None:
            continue

        if (il.get("discountable") or "").strip().lower() != "yes":
            continue  # respect POS flag

        # Line number is under TransactionLine
        try:
            line_no = int(tline.findtext("./LineNumber", "0"))
        except Exception:
            line_no = 0

        upc = (il.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        if not upc:
            continue

        qty_txt = il.findtext("SalesQuantity", il.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
        try:
            qty = int(float(qty_txt))
        except Exception:
            qty = 1

        unit_price    = _f(il.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        actual_price  = _f(il.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", "0"))
        regular_price = _f(il.findtext("RegularSellPrice", "0"))

        if qty > 0:
            lines.append({
                "line_no": line_no,
                "upc": upc,
                "qty": qty,
                "unit_price": unit_price,
                "actual_price": actual_price,
                "regular_price": regular_price,
            })
    return lines

def current_unit_price(l):
    """
    Resolve the effective per-unit price reported by the POS for comparison.
    Order: UnitPrice -> ActualSalesPrice -> RegularSellPrice.
    """
    for key in ("unit_price", "actual_price", "regular_price"):
        v = l.get(key, 0.0)
        try:
            v = float(v)
        except Exception:
            v = 0.0
        if v and v > 0:
            return v
    return 0.0

def build_2for1_rewards_xml(root: ET.Element):
    """
    For each UPC, if total qty >= 2, discount pairs to $0.50 each (newPrice).
    Distribute discounted units across lines with that UPC.
    Best-price floor: only emit newPrice if current per-unit > $0.50.
    Returns list of <AddReward> XML strings.
    """
    lines = parse_item_lines(root)
    log(f"Eligible lines -> {lines}")  # DEBUG

    if not lines:
        return []

    # Group by UPC
    upc_to_lines = {}
    for l in lines:
        upc_to_lines.setdefault(l["upc"], []).append(l)

    add_rewards = []

    for upc, lnlist in upc_to_lines.items():
        total_qty = sum(l["qty"] for l in lnlist)
        if total_qty < N_REQUIRED:
            continue

        # Complete pairs only
        discounted_units = (total_qty // N_REQUIRED) * N_REQUIRED

        # Optional per-UPC cap
        if LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC is not None:
            discounted_units = min(discounted_units, int(LIMIT_DISCOUNTED_QTY_PER_UPC))

        if discounted_units <= 0:
            continue

        # Distribute across lines (scan order). To prefer cheapest-first, sort by unit price:
        # lnlist = sorted(lnlist, key=lambda d: current_unit_price(d))

        remaining = discounted_units
        for l in lnlist:
            if remaining <= 0:
                break

            unit_now = current_unit_price(l)

            # Best-price floor: never raise price; only lower to $0.50 if current > $0.50
            if unit_now <= PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE:
                continue

            take = min(l["qty"], remaining)
            if take <= 0:
                continue

            add_rewards.append(f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{PROMO_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>{l["line_no"]}</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>newPrice</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE:.4f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardLimit type="quantity">{int(take)}</RewardLimit>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip())

            remaining -= take

    return add_rewards

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""

    add_xmls = build_2for1_rewards_xml(root)

    rewards_block = f"<RewardActions>\n    {''.join(add_xmls)}\n  </RewardActions>" if add_xmls else "<RewardActions/>"

    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  {rewards_block}
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>2 FOR $1 APPLIED WHERE ELIGIBLE</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>2 for $1.00 promo active</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply 2-for-$1 where eligible
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()
If you want no stacking at all with existing POS promos, add this inside the for l in lnlist: loop before emitting <AddReward>:

# Skip if POS already discounted (Actual < Regular)
if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
    continue
Or if you want force override (always push to $0.50 whenever pairs exist), remove the best-price check:

# Remove this line to force override
# if unit_now <= PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE: continue
You said:
so for this code, if i have two celsius products, that have a promo already, what will this code do? override it? stack it?
ChatGPT said:
With the current (fixed) code, the behavior is “best-price floor to $0.50”:

If two of the same Celsius UPC are in the live cart and each unit’s current price after the store promo is >$0.50, your server sends a line-level newPrice=0.50 for the qualifying units → effectively overrides the store promo down to $0.50.

If the store promo already made a unit ≤$0.50, your code does nothing for that unit (it won’t raise price or double-discount below $0.50).

It does not “erase” the POS promo line; it just applies a loyalty price override so the final unit price ends up at $0.50 (you may see both POS promo info and a loyalty reward on the receipt/audit).

Quick outcomes:

Promo price $2.75 → loyalty sets $0.50 (override down).

Promo price $0.75 → loyalty sets $0.50 (override down).

Promo price $0.49 → no loyalty change.

Want no stacking at all with store promos? Add this inside the reward loop before emitting <AddReward>:

# Skip if POS already discounted (Actual < Regular)
if l["actual_price"] and l["regular_price"] and l["actual_price"] < l["regular_price"]:
    continue
Want loyalty to always force $0.50 (even if POS made it cheaper)? Remove the best-price check:

# Remove this line to force override
# if unit_now <= PER_UNIT_NEW_PRICE: continue

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 13s
Yes—it worked exactly right. Your logs show the 2-for-$1 fired and overrode the two Celsius (UPC 889392001747) to $0.50 each:

In the first GetRewardsRequest at 10:01:44, my debug prints:

Eligible lines -> [{'line_no': 1, ... 'actual_price': 3.19}, {'line_no': 4, ... 'actual_price': 3.19}]
and your response includes two <AddReward> entries:

RewardTargetLineNumber 1 → newPrice 0.5000, limit 1

RewardTargetLineNumber 4 → newPrice 0.5000, limit 1

On the very next GetRewardsRequest (10:01:47) the POS echoes back both promos on each line:

a store mixAndMatchOffer, and

a loyaltyOffer with LoyaltyRewardID=DEMO-2FOR1 (this is your override to $0.50).
It also shows ActualSalesPrice switched to 0 (Gilbarco’s way of representing a promo’d line), confirming the price override path.

In FinalizeRewardsRequest (10:01:49), lines 1 and 4 again show the loyaltyOffer with large PromotionAmounts (~2.74/2.75). That’s the POS’ discount accounting (it may include rounding and tax recompute), but the key part is: the loyalty reward is recorded and the final unit price is $0.50.

So: two of the same Celsius in the live cart → we sent newPrice=$0.50 per unit → Passport applied it. Because the code uses a best-price floor, it overrides down to $0.50 when current price is above $0.50; if a store promo ever made it ≤$0.50, we would skip lowering further.

If you want to block stacking whenever a store promo is present, I can give you the one-line guard to skip lines already on sale. Or, if you want “mix flavors” (item-group / multi-UPC) next, we can flip this to a group rule.

You said:
currently it is set to give the better promo?
ChatGPT said:
Yes. With the version you’re running, it’s effectively “best price wins (floor = $0.50)”.

Quick truth table for each qualifying unit (same UPC, pairs, normal, discountable):

POS unit price > $0.50 → we override to $0.50 (loyalty is better).

POS unit price = $0.50 → do nothing (already best).

POS unit price < $0.50 → do nothing (store promo is better).

So you always end up paying the lower of the store price and $0.50—never higher, never double-discounting below $0.50.

You said:
for the items does passport use entire upc for the celsius / other products?
ChatGPT said:
Short answer: yes—Passport sends you the full item code it rang, and you should match on that exact value.

From your logs:

Celsius shows ItemCode/POSCodeFormat="upcA" with a 12-digit POSCode like 889392001747 (full UPC-A).

Manual items (like your Cup of Ice) come as POSCodeFormat="plu" with the PLU value (e.g., 16794).

Tips so your matching is bulletproof:

Treat POSCode as a string (don’t cast to int) so you never drop leading zeros.

Use both POSCodeFormat and POSCode when comparing (e.g., ("upcA","889392001747")), since the same digits in a different format (UPC-E, EAN-13, PLU) shouldn’t match by accident.

For “mix flavors” deals, put all allowed UPC-A codes into your item group. Don’t expect different flavors to share the same code.

If you ever see upcE or ean13 from a scanner, either:

include those exact codes in your group, or

normalize them to UPC-A before matching (only if you implement a reliable converter).

If you want, I can add a tiny helper that normalizes (POSCodeFormat, POSCode) to a canonical string so your item groups stay consistent and safe from leading-zero issues.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Birdies Loyalty Edge Agent
-------------------------
• Binds to local IP on port 9000 to handle Passport POS protocol
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE at the register
• Sends heartbeat pings to Birdies backend server
• Tracks online status for store location
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import time
import requests
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
# Local POS connection settings
HOST = "10.96.10.175"   # Your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000             # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

# Store identification
PDI_STORE_NUMBER = "1340"  # Your PDI store number (without leading zeros)
POS_ID = "24379"           # Your POS ID from location settings
POS_TYPE = "Passport"      # Passport or Verifone

# Backend server settings
BACKEND_URL = "https://salmanloyalty.replit.app"  # Your Replit backend URL
HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL = 15  # Send heartbeat every 15 seconds

# Edge agent info
EDGE_VERSION = "birdies-edge-1.0"
VENDOR_NAME = "BirdiesLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Session state
current_customer = None
declined_rewards = set()  # Track declined reward IDs for current transaction

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent to POS:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Received from POS:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Backend Heartbeat
# =========================
def send_heartbeat(pos_ip: str = None):
    """Send heartbeat to backend server"""
    try:
        payload = {
            "pdiStoreNumber": PDI_STORE_NUMBER,
            "posId": POS_ID,
            "posType": POS_TYPE,
            "posIpAddress": pos_ip or EXPECTED_POS_IP,
            "edgeIpAddress": HOST,
            "edgeVersion": EDGE_VERSION
        }
        
        response = requests.post(
            f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/heartbeat",
            json=payload,
            timeout=5
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            log(f"✓ Heartbeat sent to backend (Store {PDI_STORE_NUMBER})")
        else:
            log(f"⚠ Heartbeat failed: {response.status_code}")
            
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Heartbeat error: {e}")

def heartbeat_loop():
    """Background thread that sends periodic heartbeats"""
    while True:
        send_heartbeat()
        time.sleep(HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL)

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def extract_line_items(root: ET.Element):
    """Extract all product line items from transaction XML"""
    line_items = []
    items = root.findall(".//ItemLine")
    
    for item in items:
        # Try multiple UPC locations (Passport uses POSCode, others use UPC)
        upc = item.findtext(".//UPC") or item.findtext(".//POSCode") or item.findtext(".//ItemCode/POSCode") or ""
        description = item.findtext(".//Description") or ""
        quantity_elem = item.find(".//Quantity") or item.find(".//SalesQuantity")
        quantity = float(quantity_elem.text) if quantity_elem is not None and quantity_elem.text else 1.0
        amount_elem = item.find(".//SalesAmount")
        amount = float(amount_elem.text) if amount_elem is not None and amount_elem.text else 0.0
        
        if upc or amount > 0:
            line_items.append({
                "upc": upc,
                "description": description,
                "quantity": quantity,
                "amount": amount
            })
    
    return line_items

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    phone = root.findtext(".//PhoneNumber") or ""
    
    # Extract and log product line items
    line_items = extract_line_items(root)
    
    # Log transaction details only if items exist
    if line_items:
        log("=" * 60)
        log(f"🛒 TRANSACTION ITEMS ({len(line_items)} items):")
        for idx, item in enumerate(line_items, 1):
            log(f"  {idx}. UPC: {item['upc']}")
            log(f"     Desc: {item['description']}")
            log(f"     Qty: {item['quantity']}, Amount: ${item['amount']:.2f}")
        log("=" * 60)
    else:
        log("🛒 No items in transaction yet")
    
    # DEBUG: Log what we received
    log(f"DEBUG: LoyaltyID='{loyalty_id}', PhoneNumber='{phone}'")
    
    # Smart detection: If LoyaltyID looks like a phone number (10 digits), treat it as phone
    if loyalty_id and len(loyalty_id.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "")) == 10 and loyalty_id.replace("-", "").replace(" ", "").isdigit():
        log(f"Detected phone number in LoyaltyID field: {loyalty_id}")
        phone = loyalty_id
        loyalty_id = ""
    
    # Call backend to lookup customer
    try:
        payload = {}
        if loyalty_id:
            log(f"Using loyalty ID for lookup: {loyalty_id}")
            payload["loyaltyId"] = loyalty_id
        elif phone:
            log(f"Using phone number for lookup: {phone}")
            payload["phone"] = phone
        else:
            # No identifier provided
            log("⚠ No loyalty ID or phone number provided by POS")
            return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">Customer ID Required</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
        
        response = requests.post(
            f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/customer-lookup",
            json=payload,
            timeout=5
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 404:
            # Customer not found
            identifier = loyalty_id if loyalty_id else phone
            log(f"⚠ Customer not found: {identifier}")
            return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">Customer not found. Please sign up at birdies.com</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
        
        if response.status_code != 200:
            log(f"⚠ Customer lookup failed: {response.status_code}")
            return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">System Error</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
        
        customer = response.json()
        customer_id = customer.get("customerId")
        points_balance = customer.get("pointsBalance", 0)
        first_name = customer.get("firstName", "")
        last_name = customer.get("lastName", "")
        
        log(f"✓ Customer found: {first_name} {last_name} ({points_balance} pts)")
        
        # Store customer info for later use in finalize
        global current_customer
        current_customer = customer
        
        # Masked ID for display
        display_id = loyalty_id if loyalty_id else phone
        masked = (display_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if display_id else "****"
        
        # Calculate subtotal from extracted line items
        subtotal = sum(item['amount'] for item in line_items)
        log(f"Calculated subtotal: ${subtotal:.2f}")
        
        # Build reward actions - ask customer if they want to redeem points
        reward_actions = ""
        global declined_rewards
        reward_id = "BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION"
        
        # Only offer redemption if not already declined and customer has enough points
        if reward_id not in declined_rewards and subtotal > 0 and points_balance >= 100:
            # Calculate recommended redemption
            try:
                redemption_response = requests.post(
                    f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/calculate-redemption",
                    json={
                        "customerId": customer_id,
                        "eligibleSubtotal": subtotal,
                        "lineItems": line_items
                    },
                    timeout=5
                )
                
                if redemption_response.status_code == 200:
                    redemption_data = redemption_response.json()
                    recommended_amount = redemption_data.get("recommendedRedemption", 0)
                    points_to_use = recommended_amount * 100  # $1 = 100 points
                    
                    if recommended_amount > 0:
                        log(f"Recommending ${recommended_amount} redemption ({points_to_use} pts)")
                        # Add optional reward with prompt - customer must accept
                        reward_actions = f"""

      <LoyaltyRewardID>{reward_id}</LoyaltyRewardID>

      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>

      <RewardReceiptDescShort>Loyalty</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>Birdies Loyalty Redemption</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>"""
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"⚠ Redemption calculation failed: {e}")
        elif reward_id in declined_rewards:
            log(f"Skipping redemption offer - customer already declined")
        
        return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>

      </DisplayCommand>

</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
        
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Customer lookup error: {e}")
        return f"""

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    global current_customer
    
    # Extract transaction details
    transaction_id = root.findtext(".//TransactionID") or root.findtext(".//POSTransactionID") or "N/A"
    
    # Calculate eligible subtotal (exclude lottery)
    eligible_subtotal = 0.0
    items = root.findall(".//Item") or root.findall(".//LineItem")
    
    for item in items:
        price_str = item.findtext(".//ExtendedPrice") or item.findtext(".//Price") or "0"
        category = item.findtext(".//Category") or ""
        
        # Exclude lottery from eligible subtotal
        if "lottery" not in category.lower():
            try:
                eligible_subtotal += float(price_str)
            except:
                pass
    
    if not current_customer:
        log("⚠ No customer in session, skipping loyalty finalization")
        declined_rewards.clear()  # Clear for safety
        return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>Thank you for shopping at Birdies!</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()
    
    try:
        customer_id = current_customer.get("customerId")
        
        # Check if points were redeemed (look for redemption in transaction)
        points_redeemed = 0
        redemption_elements = root.findall(".//Redemption") or root.findall(".//PointsRedeemed")
        for elem in redemption_elements:
            try:
                points_redeemed += int(elem.text or "0")
            except:
                pass
        
        # Call backend to finalize transaction
        payload = {
            "customerId": customer_id,
            "eligibleSubtotal": eligible_subtotal,
            "pointsRedeemed": points_redeemed,
            "transactionId": transaction_id
        }
        
        response = requests.post(
            f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/finalize-transaction",
            json=payload,
            timeout=5
        )
        
        if response.status_code == 200:
            result = response.json()
            points_earned = result.get("pointsEarned", 0)
            new_balance = result.get("newBalance", 0)
            
            log(f"✓ Transaction finalized: Earned {points_earned} pts, New balance: {new_balance} pts")
            
            # Build receipt lines
            receipt_lines = []
            if points_redeemed > 0:
                receipt_lines.append(f"Points Redeemed: {points_redeemed} pts (${points_redeemed // 100})")
            receipt_lines.append(f"Points Earned: {points_earned} pts")
            receipt_lines.append(f"New Balance: {new_balance} pts")
            receipt_lines.append("Thank you for shopping at Birdies!")
            
            receipt_xml = "\n".join([f"      <ReceiptLine>{line}</ReceiptLine>" for line in receipt_lines])
            
            # Clear customer session and declined rewards
            current_customer = None
            declined_rewards.clear()
            
            return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
{receipt_xml}
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()
        else:
            log(f"⚠ Finalize transaction failed: {response.status_code}")
            current_customer = None
            declined_rewards.clear()
            return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>Thank you for shopping at Birdies!</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()
            
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Finalize error: {e}")
        current_customer = None
        declined_rewards.clear()
        return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>Thank you for shopping at Birdies!</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_redemption_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    """Handle customer declining a redemption offer"""
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_reward_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyRewardID") or "UNKNOWN"
    
    global declined_rewards
    declined_rewards.add(loyalty_reward_id)
    
    log(f"✗ Customer declined redemption: {loyalty_reward_id}")
    # Return empty response - no action needed
    return f"<CancelRedemptionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelRedemptionResponse>"

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    global current_customer, declined_rewards
    current_customer = None  # Clear customer session on cancel
    declined_rewards.clear()  # Clear declined rewards for next transaction
    log("Transaction cancelled, customer session cleared")
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>Welcome to Birdies Loyalty!</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"POS connected from {peer}")

    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"⚠ Rejecting unexpected POS IP: {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    # Send heartbeat when POS connects
    send_heartbeat(addr[0])

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"POS disconnected: {peer}")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"Bad header from {peer}: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"POS heartbeat from {peer}")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"Payload CRC/length mismatch from {peer}")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"XML parse error from {peer}: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # Handle POS messages
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))
                send_heartbeat(addr[0])  # Update backend when POS checks status

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelRedemptionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_redemption_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                log(f"{tag} received (no response required)")

            else:
                log(f"⚠ Unhandled message: {tag}")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"POS timeout: {peer}")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"POS error: {peer} - {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Birdies Loyalty Edge Agent")
    log(f"Store: {PDI_STORE_NUMBER} | POS Type: {POS_TYPE} | POS ID: {POS_ID}")
    log(f"Backend: {BACKEND_URL}")
    log(f"Listening on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    
    # Start heartbeat thread
    heartbeat_thread = threading.Thread(target=heartbeat_loop, daemon=True)
    heartbeat_thread.start()
    log("✓ Heartbeat thread started")
    
    # Start POS listener
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("\nShutting down...")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()

do you see any issues with the code itself
ChatGPT said:

Thought for 13s
Good news: the agent is solid overall (framing, CRCs, threading, heartbeats). A few things, though, will bite you in production—mostly around how you parse items and detect redemptions.

Here are the important issues and drop-in fixes:

1) Item parsing includes voids & non-discountables
Problem: extract_line_items walks //ItemLine without checking its parent TransactionLine@status="normal" and ignores discountable="no". That’s why your log showed a voided line as $0.00 “in cart”.

Fix (replace extract_line_items):

def extract_line_items(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Only count live, discountable lines for subtotal/promotions.
    Returns list of dicts: {upc, description, quantity, amount, line_no, format}
    """
    items = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        status = (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower()
        if status != "normal":
            continue

        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is None:
            continue

        # Respect discountable flag (optional: remove this if you want subtotal to include non-discountables)
        disc = (il.get("discountable") or "").strip().lower()
        # If you want subtotal for *all* items, don't gate on disc == "yes"
        # For now we keep it permissive for subtotal but capture flag for rewards logic.
        line_no = int(tline.findtext("./LineNumber", "0") or 0)
        pos_format = (il.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCodeFormat") or "").strip()
        upc = (il.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        desc = (il.findtext("./Description") or "").strip()

        # Quantity and amount (Passport usually provides both)
        qtxt = il.findtext("SalesQuantity", il.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
        qty = float(qtxt or 1.0)
        atxt = il.findtext("SalesAmount")  # extended amount for the line
        # Fallback to ActualSalesPrice * qty if SalesAmount is missing/zero
        ap = il.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", il.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        amount = float(atxt) if (atxt and atxt.strip() != "") else float(ap or 0) * qty

        items.append({
            "upc": upc,
            "format": pos_format,
            "description": desc,
            "quantity": qty,
            "amount": amount,
            "line_no": line_no,
            "discountable": disc == "yes",
        })
    return items
2) Subtotal/points use a different schema in Finalize
Problem: build_finalize_response looks for //Item or //LineItem and //ExtendedPrice, which aren’t in Passport payloads. Result: eligible_subtotal = 0, so you always log “Points Earned: 0”.

Fix (replace subtotal block in build_finalize_response):

# Calculate eligible subtotal from Passport model
eligible_subtotal = 0.0
for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
    if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
        continue
    il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
    if il is None:
        continue
    # Exclude lottery by merchandise/category if you need
    merch = (il.findtext("MerchandiseCode") or "").lower()
    desc  = (il.findtext("Description") or "").lower()
    if "lottery" in merch or "lotto" in desc:
        continue

    qtxt = il.findtext("SalesQuantity", il.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
    qty = float(qtxt or 1.0)
    atxt = il.findtext("SalesAmount")
    ap = il.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", il.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
    amount = float(atxt) if (atxt and atxt.strip() != "") else float(ap or 0) * qty
    eligible_subtotal += amount
3) Detecting accepted redemptions
Problem: You look for <Redemption>/<PointsRedeemed> tags, which Passport doesn’t send. Basket loyalty usually appears as:

TransactionLine/TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID (transaction-level), or

ItemLine/Promotion[...] with LoyaltyRewardID (item-level).

Fix (replace your “points_redeemed” detection):

# Detect accepted redemption by presence of our reward in TenderInfo or Promotions
points_redeemed = 0
reward_id = "BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION"

# Transaction-level tender path
for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
    ti = tline.find("./TenderInfo")
    if ti is not None:
        lrid = (ti.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip()
        if lrid == reward_id:
            # Use TenderAmount as the redeemed dollars, convert to points (100 pts = $1)
            tamt_txt = ti.findtext("TenderAmount", "0")
            try:
                dollars = float(tamt_txt or 0)
                points_redeemed += int(round(dollars * 100))
            except:
                pass

    il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
    if il is not None:
        for promo in il.findall("./Promotion"):
            lrid = (promo.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip()
            if lrid == reward_id:
                # For item-level, sum PromotionAmount to dollars, convert to points
                pam = promo.findtext("PromotionAmount", "0")
                try:
                    dollars = float(pam or 0)
                    points_redeemed += int(round(dollars * 100))
                except:
                    pass
4) Optional prompts vs auto-apply
Right now you send:

<InstantRewardFlag value="no"/>
<RewardPromptTextShort>Redeem $1.00 for 100 pts?</RewardPromptTextShort>
This requires the cashier to accept on the POS. If you want auto-apply (no prompt), set InstantRewardFlag="yes" and drop the prompt line.

5) Phone detection & masking
Your phone detection is fine, but small hardening:

digits = "".join(ch for ch in loyalty_id if ch.isdigit())
if len(digits) == 10:
    phone = digits
    loyalty_id = ""
This handles spaces, dashes, parentheses.

6) Double “CancelRedemptionRequest” branch
You both handle and log-ignore CancelRedemptionRequest:

elif tag == "CancelRedemptionRequest": send_xml(...)

elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest","EndCustomerRequest","CancelRedemptionRequest"):
    log(...)
The second tuple is redundant; it won’t break anything (first one wins), but you can remove CancelRedemptionRequest from the tuple to avoid confusion.

7) Resilience & timeouts
requests.post(..., timeout=5) is good. Consider timeout=(3,5) for connect/read split.

You might want a requests.Session() if you expect high QPS (reuse TCP).

8) Consistent UPC handling
If you plan to do item-group promos later, store (format, upc) together. E.g.:

key = f"{item['format']}:{item['upc']}"
This avoids collisions (e.g., PLU “12345” ≠ UPC-A “00000012345”).

TL;DR patch list
✅ Replace extract_line_items with the “status=normal” version above.

✅ Fix build_finalize_response to compute subtotal from TransactionLine/ItemLine.

✅ Detect accepted redemptions via TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID and Promotion/LoyaltyRewardID.

✅ Decide prompt vs auto-apply (InstantRewardFlag).

✅ Remove redundant CancelRedemptionRequest in the “no response” tuple.

✅ (Optional) Harden phone parsing & switch to requests.Session().

If you want, I can roll these into a single updated file and include a toggle:

AUTO_APPLY_REDEMPTION = True  # or False for prompt
…so you can flip behavior without editing XML strings.

You sent the reward with <InstantRewardFlag value="no"> and a prompt:

That means the POS must accept it. In your log, there’s no acceptance (no tender with your LoyaltyRewardID and no reduced totals), so the discount wasn’t applied.

There are two secondary things to be aware of (they didn’t cause this miss, but they matter next):

Your item/subtotal parsing was previously lenient (counted void lines), which can skew the recommended redemption amount. You should filter to TransactionLine status="normal".

If you prefer to keep it optional (prompted), leave InstantRewardFlag="no" but know the cashier has to accept it; otherwise nothing applies.
