#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server
-------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• $1.00 OFF any transaction when ANY LoyaltyID (e.g., phone #) is entered
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"   # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000             # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Hard-coded reward: $1.00 off transaction total (line 0)
DISCOUNT_VALUE = 1.00
REWARD_ID      = "DEMO-1OFF"
RECEIPT_SHORT  = "$1OFF"            # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG   = "Loyalty $1 Off"   # <= 24 chars

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""
    # Transaction-level reward (line 0), amountOff = $1.00, instant apply.
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>LOYALTY $1 OFF APPLIED</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>$1 Loyalty discount available</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply $1 off
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 6s
Primary cause = the prompt.

In the Edge Agent run you shared, the $1 offer was sent as an optional redemption:

InstantRewardFlag was "no"

RewardPromptTextShort asked: “Redeem $1.00 for 100 pts?”

Result: the POS required a cashier acceptance. In your log, there’s no acceptance event: the follow-up FinalizeRewardsRequest has no TenderInfo line carrying your LoyaltyRewardID and totals weren’t reduced → the discount didn’t apply.

How we know from the log:

After the prompt, there’s no tender like:

<TenderInfo>
  <TenderCode>loyaltyOffer</TenderCode>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <TenderAmount>1.00</TenderAmount>
</TenderInfo>
Nor any item‐level <Promotion ... LoyaltyRewardID="BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION">.

By contrast, your hardcoded demo (the one you pasted at the end) sets:

<InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
<RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
<RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
<RewardValue>1.00</RewardValue>
So it auto-applies without a prompt—and that “just works.”

Secondary things (not the blocker here, but worth fixing):

Your finalize code wasn’t checking the Passport way of recording accepted loyalty (look for TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID or item-level Promotion/LoyaltyRewardID). So even if a cashier had accepted, your agent might still log “0 pts redeemed.”

Earlier parsing included void lines; you already saw how that can skew subtotals.

What to do
If you want the $1 to apply automatically every time: keep InstantRewardFlag="yes" (no RewardPromptTextShort).

If you want it optional: keep InstantRewardFlag="no", but train cashiers to accept the prompt—and add finalize detection of:

TransactionLine/TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID == BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION (use TenderAmount as redeemed dollars), or

item-level Promotion entries with your LoyaltyRewardID.

Bottom line: in the failed case, the prompt (Instant=no) + no acceptance is the exact reason the discount didn’t apply.

Thought for 6s
Almost there—this time your GetRewardsResponse is set to auto-apply:

You sent <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/> ✅

Basket discount: RewardTargetLineNumber=0, RewardDiscountMethod=amountOff, RewardValue=1.00 ✅

So the offer is correct and shouldn’t require cashier acceptance.

Two notes about what the log shows (and doesn’t):

You still include <RewardPromptTextShort>…</RewardPromptTextShort> even with InstantRewardFlag="yes". Passport typically ignores the prompt when Instant is yes, but to be 100% clean you can remove that line.

There’s no follow-up FinalizeRewardsRequest (or a second GetRewards echo with a tender) after 12:07:27. Without a finalize we can’t see the tender the POS creates (e.g., a TransactionLine/TenderInfo carrying your LoyaltyRewardID=BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION)—so the log can’t confirm application yet.

What to do to confirm
Keep InstantRewardFlag="yes" and remove the prompt line.

Ring a quick sale again and proceed to total/checkout so the POS sends FinalizeRewardsRequest.

In your agent, log in Finalize whether you see:

TransactionLine/TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID == BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION (use TenderAmount as the redeemed $), or

any item-level <Promotion … LoyaltyRewardID="BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION">.

If you want, I can give you the exact Finalize snippet to print out detected tender/promo and the redeemed amount in dollars/points.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Gilbarco Passport Loyalty Demo Server
-------------------------------------
• Binds to 10.96.10.175:9000
• Implements POSLOYALTY 28-byte header + XML framing
• Prints every inbound/outbound XML in the terminal
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE (OnlineStatus)
• $1.00 OFF any transaction when ANY LoyaltyID (e.g., phone #) is entered
• Handles common messages: OnlineStatus, GetRewards, FinalizeRewards, CancelTransaction,
  GetCustomerMessaging, Begin/EndCustomer (info only), EndPeriod (ack so POS stops retrying)
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
HOST = "10.96.10.175"   # your PC's Passport NIC IP
PORT = 9000             # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Optional safety: reject any other source. Set to None to allow all.

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Hard-coded reward: $1.00 off transaction total (line 0)
DISCOUNT_VALUE = 1.00
REWARD_ID      = "DEMO-1OFF"
RECEIPT_SHORT  = "$1OFF"            # <= 8 chars
RECEIPT_LONG   = "Loyalty $1 Off"   # <= 24 chars

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    # pretty print outbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent XML:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    # pretty print inbound
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Recv XML:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    loyalty_id = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or ""
    masked = (loyalty_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if loyalty_id else ""
    # Transaction-level reward (line 0), amountOff = $1.00, instant apply.
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions>
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{DISCOUNT_VALUE:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>{RECEIPT_SHORT}</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>{RECEIPT_LONG}</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>LOYALTY $1 OFF APPLIED</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>$1 Loyalty discount available</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"Connection from {peer}")

    # Optional safety: only accept the Passport IP you observed
    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"Rejecting unexpected host {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"{peer} closed connection.")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} bad header: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                # Consume optional heartbeat payload if present
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"{peer} heartbeat")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"{peer} payload CRC/length mismatch")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"{peer} XML parse error: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            # ---- Message routing ----
            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                # Any LoyaltyID is treated as valid; apply $1 off
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                # Acknowledge so POS stops resending
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest", "CancelRedemptionRequest"):
                # Informational only — no response required
                log(f"{peer} {tag} received (no response required).")

            else:
                log(f"{peer} Unhandled message type: {tag} (no response sent).")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"{peer} timed out.")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"{peer} error: {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Loyalty server on {HOST}:{PORT}")
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)
    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("Shutting down.")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()

this was the code, but what was the difference between the way this did it and the edge code
ChatGPT said:
Great question. Here’s the real diff between the demo server you just ran and your Edge Agent that made the behavior feel different.

What the demo does (your last script)
Always applies $1 off whenever a LoyaltyID is present.

Sends a basket reward with:

InstantRewardFlag="yes"

RewardTargetLineNumber=0

RewardDiscountMethod=amountOff

RewardValue=1.00

No prompt text, so no cashier action is needed.

No backend logic (no points, no eligibility checks).

Doesn’t try to verify acceptance in Finalize; it just prints “LOYALTY $1 OFF APPLIED”.

Result: if the transaction proceeds to tender, the POS will create a loyalty tender automatically.

What the Edge Agent does
Conditional offer (depends on backend/points/cart):

Looks up the customer → calculates a recommended redemption.

Only offers if points ≥ 100 and subtotal > 0.

Originally you had InstantRewardFlag="no" + prompt → cashier had to accept.

In your later run you set InstantRewardFlag="yes" (good), but still left a RewardPromptTextShort in the XML. Passport ignores it with Instant=yes, but leaving it is confusing—just remove it.

Edge Agent also had parsing/finalize nuances:

Item parsing initially included void lines (could skew subtotal / recommendations).

Finalize didn’t check how Passport records acceptance (no scan for TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID or item Promotion/LoyaltyRewardID), so your logs couldn’t confirm redemptions even if they did apply.

It also tracked declined rewards (so a previously declined offer wouldn’t be re-shown).

Why the demo “works” even when Edge looked like it didn’t
The demo sends an unconditional, instantaneous $1 basket discount with no prompt and no backend dependencies. If you don’t cancel the sale, the POS will record the loyalty tender.

The Edge run that didn’t “apply” earlier was because it was prompted (Instant=no) and the cashier didn’t accept; later you switched to Instant=yes but canceled the transaction before tender → nothing to show in Finalize.

If you want Edge to behave like the demo (always $1 off)
In build_get_rewards_response of the Edge Agent:

Skip backend checks and send:

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <!-- remove RewardPromptTextShort entirely -->
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.00</RewardValue>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>Loyalty</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>Birdies Loyalty Redemption</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Filter items to TransactionLine status="normal" only (just to keep logs/subtotals sane).

In Finalize, log acceptance by reading:

TransactionLine/TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID == BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION (use TenderAmount), and/or

any ItemLine/Promotion with your LoyaltyRewardID.

TL;DR
Core difference: demo = instant, unconditional, no prompt; Edge = conditional, (used to be) prompted, backend/points-driven.

The failure before was the prompt (Instant=no). The latest Edge attempt was fine (Instant=yes) but you canceled before tender, so you couldn’t see the $1 on Finalize.

Remove the prompt line when Instant=yes, and either finish the sale to see the tender—or change Edge to unconditional if that’s the desired behavior.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 14s
You’ve got it: both programs run locally and both “speak Passport.” The only functional difference is that the Edge agent does a couple of HTTP lookups before it decides what to send. That’s what can make the POS feel like it’s “buffering.”

Here’s what I see, and how to make Edge behave exactly like the hard-coded server:

What was correct
In your Edge log at 12:07:27 you did send a proper, automatic $1 basket reward:

InstantRewardFlag="yes", RewardTargetLineNumber=0, amountOff=1.00. ✅

John Doe had 175 pts, and your backend recommended $1.00; Edge built the right XML.

Why the POS can look “stuck” with Edge (but not with the demo)
Edge waits on the backend before replying.

Even if it’s only ~1–2 seconds, Passport’s UI shows “Processing loyalty…” while it waits for your GetRewardsResponse. The demo never waits (no HTTP), so it feels instant.

If the backend takes longer (DNS hiccup, TLS, cold start), the spinner lingers.

Prompt text included with Instant="yes"

You sent RewardPromptTextShort and InstantRewardFlag="yes". Many POS builds ignore the prompt when Instant=yes, but some UI skins still show a tiny modal or spinner artifact. It’s harmless—but can look like buffering. Just remove the prompt line when Instant=yes.

No tender/Finalize yet

With either server, the $1 “applies” as a loyalty tender when the cashier totals out. If you cancel or don’t proceed to tender, you won’t see the applied discount on Finalize—so it looks like “nothing happened.”

Make Edge feel as snappy as the demo
Remove the prompt when Instant=yes

<AddReward>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
  <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
  <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
  <RewardValue>1.00</RewardValue>
  <RewardReceiptDescShort>Loyalty</RewardReceiptDescShort>
  <RewardReceiptDescLong>Birdies Loyalty Redemption</RewardReceiptDescLong>
</AddReward>
Cap backend latency & fail open fast

Use aggressive timeouts (e.g., timeout=(2, 2)) and wrap calls in try/except.

If lookup/redemption calc fails or exceeds your budget, still reply immediately (e.g., with no reward or a default $0) so the POS isn’t left waiting.

Log elapsed time for your GetRewardsRequest path so you can see when responses exceed ~1s.

Filter to live lines when computing subtotal
Only count TransactionLine status="normal" so your recommended redemption is stable and Edge doesn’t spend time second-guessing void lines.

Confirm application at Finalize
In FinalizeRewardsRequest, log if you see:

TransactionLine/TenderInfo/LoyaltyRewardID=BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION (use TenderAmount), or

item Promotion entries with your LoyaltyRewardID.
That verifies the $1 actually hit the check.

Bottom line
Yes, Edge still runs locally; the only extra moving part is the database/points check.

Your build was correct (Instant=yes; $1 off).

The “buffering” you felt is just the round-trip to your backend (plus the leftover prompt text). Trim the prompt, keep HTTP timeouts tight with a quick fallback, and the Edge agent will feel identical to the hard-coded server—in behavior and in what the POS receives.

def _detect_loyalty_tender_or_promos(root: ET.Element, reward_id: str):
    dollars = 0.0
    # Transaction-level tender
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        ti = tline.find("./TenderInfo")
        if ti is not None and (ti.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip() == reward_id:
            try:
                dollars += float(ti.findtext("TenderAmount", "0") or 0)
            except: pass
        # Item-level promos (rare for basket, but safe to check)
        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is not None:
            for promo in il.findall("./Promotion"):
                if (promo.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip() == reward_id:
                    try:
                        dollars += float(promo.findtext("PromotionAmount", "0") or 0)
                    except: pass
    return round(dollars, 2)

Summary
You do not need to change your server to fix the “buffering” feel.

Make Edge send the same instant $1 XML (no prompt), cap HTTP timeouts, and reply quickly even on backend hiccups.

Finish a transaction; in Finalize you’ll see the TenderInfo with your LoyaltyRewardID and TenderAmount=1.00, proving it applied.

You said:
rewrite the entire edge code
ChatGPT said:
Here’s a cleaned-up, production-ready Birdies Loyalty Edge Agent that:

replies instantly with a $1 basket discount when eligible (no prompt),

keeps the POS UI snappy with tight HTTP timeouts + fail-open,

parses only live (status="normal") item lines for stable subtotals,

logs and verifies redemption on Finalize by detecting Passport’s loyalty tender,

hardens phone/ID parsing, and

removes small logic duplicates.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
Birdies Loyalty Edge Agent (Instant $1 Basket Redemption)
--------------------------------------------------------
• Listens on 10.96.10.175:9000 to handle Passport POSLOYALTY protocol
• Keeps loyalty ONLINE at the register
• Sends periodic heartbeats to the Birdies backend
• Looks up customer/points, calculates a recommended redemption
• When eligible, auto-applies a $1 basket discount (transaction-level tender)
• Confirms redemption on Finalize by detecting Passport tender/promotions
"""

import socket
import threading
import datetime
import binascii
import struct
import uuid
import time
import requests
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from xml.dom import minidom

# =========================
# Configuration
# =========================
# Local POS connection
HOST = "10.96.10.175"    # Edge NIC IP (Passport-facing)
PORT = 9000              # Loyalty port configured on Passport MWS
EXPECTED_POS_IP = "10.5.50.2"   # Reject other sources. Set to None to allow all.

# Store identification
PDI_STORE_NUMBER = "1340"
POS_ID = "24379"
POS_TYPE = "Passport"

# Backend
BACKEND_URL = "https://salmanloyalty.replit.app"
HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL = 15  # seconds

# Edge info
EDGE_VERSION = "birdies-edge-1.1"
VENDOR_NAME = "BirdiesLoyalty"
VENDOR_VER  = "1.0"
IFACE_VER   = "1.0"

# Redemption config
REWARD_ID = "BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION"
REWARD_VALUE_DOLLARS = 1.00     # $1 off basket
POINTS_PER_DOLLAR = 100         # 100 pts = $1
AUTO_APPLY_REDEMPTION = True    # send Instant=yes and NO prompt

# HTTP session & timeouts
SESSION = requests.Session()
REQUEST_TIMEOUT = (2, 2)        # (connect, read) seconds

# Session state
current_customer = None
declined_rewards = set()  # tracked per txn if you enable prompted flows later

# POSLOYALTY framing
SIGNATURE = b"POSLOYALTY\x00\x00"
ACTION_MESSAGE   = 1
ACTION_HEARTBEAT = 2

# =========================
# Utilities
# =========================
def log(msg: str):
    ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
    print(f"[{ts}] {msg}", flush=True)

def crc32(b: bytes) -> int:
    return binascii.crc32(b) & 0xFFFFFFFF

def pack_header(xml_bytes: bytes, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE) -> bytes:
    data_len = len(xml_bytes)
    chk_data = crc32(xml_bytes)
    head_wo_hdr_crc = SIGNATURE + struct.pack("<III", action, data_len, chk_data)
    chk_hdr = crc32(head_wo_hdr_crc)
    return head_wo_hdr_crc + struct.pack("<I", chk_hdr)

def send_xml(conn: socket.socket, xml_str: str, action: int = ACTION_MESSAGE):
    xml_bytes = xml_str.encode("utf-8")
    hdr = pack_header(xml_bytes, action)
    conn.sendall(hdr + xml_bytes)
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = xml_str
    log("→ Sent to POS:\n" + pretty)

def recv_exact(conn: socket.socket, n: int) -> bytes:
    buf = b""
    while len(buf) < n:
        chunk = conn.recv(n - len(buf))
        if not chunk:
            return b""
        buf += chunk
    return buf

def parse_header(hdr: bytes):
    if len(hdr) != 28:
        raise ValueError("short header")
    if hdr[:12] != SIGNATURE:
        raise ValueError("bad signature")
    action, data_len, chk_data, chk_hdr = struct.unpack("<IIII", hdr[12:28])
    if crc32(hdr[:24]) != chk_hdr:
        raise ValueError("header CRC mismatch")
    return action, data_len, chk_data

def parse_xml(xml_bytes: bytes):
    raw = xml_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    try:
        pretty = minidom.parseString(xml_bytes).toprettyxml()
    except Exception:
        pretty = raw
    log("← Received from POS:\n" + pretty)
    root = ET.fromstring(raw)
    return root, raw

def get_req_ids(root: ET.Element):
    pos_seq = root.findtext(".//POSSequenceID") or "POSSEQ"
    loy_seq = root.findtext(".//LoyaltySequenceID")
    if not loy_seq or not loy_seq.strip():
        loy_seq = "LOY-" + uuid.uuid4().hex[:12].upper()
    return pos_seq, loy_seq

def resp_header(pos_seq: str, loy_seq: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"<ResponseHeader>"
        f"<POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>{IFACE_VER}</POSLoyaltyInterfaceVersion>"
        f"<VendorName>{VENDOR_NAME}</VendorName>"
        f"<VendorModelVersion>{VENDOR_VER}</VendorModelVersion>"
        f"<POSSequenceID>{pos_seq}</POSSequenceID>"
        f"<LoyaltySequenceID>{loy_seq}</LoyaltySequenceID>"
        f"</ResponseHeader>"
    )

# =========================
# Backend Heartbeat
# =========================
def send_heartbeat(pos_ip: str = None):
    try:
        payload = {
            "pdiStoreNumber": PDI_STORE_NUMBER,
            "posId": POS_ID,
            "posType": POS_TYPE,
            "posIpAddress": pos_ip or EXPECTED_POS_IP,
            "edgeIpAddress": HOST,
            "edgeVersion": EDGE_VERSION
        }
        r = SESSION.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/heartbeat", json=payload, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            log(f"✓ Heartbeat sent to backend (Store {PDI_STORE_NUMBER})")
        else:
            log(f"⚠ Heartbeat failed: {r.status_code}")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Heartbeat error: {e}")

def heartbeat_loop():
    while True:
        send_heartbeat()
        time.sleep(HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL)

# =========================
# POS Helpers
# =========================
def _only_live_item_lines(root: ET.Element):
    """
    Extracts only 'normal' TransactionLine/ItemLine entries.
    Returns list of dicts usable for logging/subtotal logic.
    """
    items = []
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        if (tline.get("status") or "").strip().lower() != "normal":
            continue
        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is None:
            continue
        upc = (il.findtext("./ItemCode/POSCode") or "").strip()
        desc = (il.findtext("./Description") or "").strip()
        qtxt = il.findtext("SalesQuantity", il.findtext("SellingUnits", "1"))
        qty = float(qtxt or 1.0)
        atxt = il.findtext("SalesAmount")
        ap = il.findtext("ActualSalesPrice", il.findtext("UnitPrice", "0"))
        amount = float(atxt) if (atxt and atxt.strip()) else float(ap or 0) * qty
        items.append({"upc": upc, "description": desc, "quantity": qty, "amount": amount})
    return items

def _detect_loyalty_tender_or_promos(root: ET.Element, reward_id: str) -> float:
    """
    Sums dollars applied as loyalty tender (TransactionLine/TenderInfo) or
    as item-level promotions where LoyaltyRewardID == reward_id.
    """
    dollars = 0.0
    for tline in root.findall(".//TransactionDetailGroup/TransactionLine"):
        ti = tline.find("./TenderInfo")
        if ti is not None:
            lrid = (ti.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip()
            if lrid == reward_id:
                try:
                    dollars += float(ti.findtext("TenderAmount", "0") or 0)
                except:
                    pass
        il = tline.find("./ItemLine")
        if il is not None:
            for promo in il.findall("./Promotion"):
                lrid = (promo.findtext("LoyaltyRewardID") or "").strip()
                if lrid == reward_id:
                    try:
                        dollars += float(promo.findtext("PromotionAmount", "0") or 0)
                    except:
                        pass
    return round(dollars, 2)

# =========================
# Response Builders
# =========================
def build_online_status_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return (
        "<GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
        f"{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}"
        '<PromptForLoyaltyFlag value="yes"/>'
        "</GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusResponse>"
    )

def build_get_rewards_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)

    # Extract loyalty ID / phone (hardened)
    loyalty_id = (root.findtext(".//LoyaltyID") or "").strip()
    phone = (root.findtext(".//PhoneNumber") or "").strip()
    digits = "".join(ch for ch in loyalty_id if ch.isdigit())
    if len(digits) == 10:
        phone = digits
        loyalty_id = ""

    # Extract items (live only) and log
    items = _only_live_item_lines(root)
    if items:
        log("=" * 60)
        log(f"🛒 TRANSACTION ITEMS ({len(items)} items):")
        for idx, it in enumerate(items, 1):
            log(f"  {idx}. UPC: {it['upc']}")
            log(f"     Desc: {it['description']}")
            log(f"     Qty: {it['quantity']}, Amount: ${it['amount']:.2f}")
        log("=" * 60)
    else:
        log("🛒 No live items in transaction yet")

    log(f"DEBUG: LoyaltyID='{loyalty_id}', PhoneNumber='{phone}'")

    # Lookup customer (fail-open fast so POS isn't stuck)
    try:
        payload = {}
        if loyalty_id:
            log(f"Using loyalty ID for lookup: {loyalty_id}")
            payload["loyaltyId"] = loyalty_id
        elif phone:
            log(f"Using phone number for lookup: {phone}")
            payload["phone"] = phone
        else:
            log("⚠ No loyalty ID or phone number provided by POS")
            return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">Customer ID Required</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions/>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

        r = SESSION.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/customer-lookup", json=payload, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Customer lookup error: {e}")
        # Fail-open: reply quickly; POS UI shouldn't hang
        return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">System Busy</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions/>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

    if r.status_code == 404:
        ident = loyalty_id or phone
        log(f"⚠ Customer not found: {ident}")
        return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">Customer not found. Please sign up at birdies.com</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions/>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()
    if r.status_code != 200:
        log(f"⚠ Customer lookup failed: {r.status_code}")
        return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="no">System Error</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <RewardActions/>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

    customer = r.json()
    global current_customer
    current_customer = customer

    customer_id = customer.get("customerId")
    points_balance = int(customer.get("pointsBalance", 0))
    first_name = customer.get("firstName", "")
    last_name  = customer.get("lastName", "")

    log(f"✓ Customer found: {first_name} {last_name} ({points_balance} pts)")

    # Masked display ID
    display_id = (loyalty_id or phone or "")
    masked = (display_id[-4:].rjust(10, "*")) if display_id else "****"

    subtotal = round(sum(it["amount"] for it in items), 2)
    log(f"Calculated subtotal: ${subtotal:.2f}")

    # Decide redemption (backend calculator optional)
    recommended_amount = 0.0
    if subtotal > 0 and points_balance >= POINTS_PER_DOLLAR:
        # You can keep this server calc, but we keep fail-open and cap to $1 here.
        try:
            rd = SESSION.post(
                f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/calculate-redemption",
                json={"customerId": customer_id, "eligibleSubtotal": subtotal, "lineItems": items},
                timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT
            )
            if rd.status_code == 200:
                rec = float(rd.json().get("recommendedRedemption", 0) or 0)
                # For this flow, clamp to $1.00 for consistency with demo
                recommended_amount = min(REWARD_VALUE_DOLLARS, round(rec, 2))
        except Exception as e:
            log(f"⚠ Redemption calc error: {e}")

    reward_actions_xml = ""
    if AUTO_APPLY_REDEMPTION and recommended_amount >= REWARD_VALUE_DOLLARS:
        # Send the SAME instant reward XML as the working demo (no prompt)
        reward_actions_xml = f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="yes"/>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{REWARD_VALUE_DOLLARS:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>Loyalty</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>Birdies Loyalty Redemption</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip()
        log(f"→ Applying instant ${REWARD_VALUE_DOLLARS:.2f} basket redemption")
    elif recommended_amount > 0:
        # If you ever want an operator prompt instead of instant, flip AUTO_APPLY_REDEMPTION=False
        pts = int(recommended_amount * POINTS_PER_DOLLAR)
        reward_actions_xml = f"""
    <AddReward>
      <LoyaltyRewardID>{REWARD_ID}</LoyaltyRewardID>
      <InstantRewardFlag value="no"/>
      <RewardPromptTextShort>Redeem ${recommended_amount:.2f} for {pts} pts?</RewardPromptTextShort>
      <RewardTargetLineNumber>0</RewardTargetLineNumber>
      <RewardDiscountMethod>amountOff</RewardDiscountMethod>
      <RewardValue>{recommended_amount:.2f}</RewardValue>
      <RewardReceiptDescShort>Loyalty</RewardReceiptDescShort>
      <RewardReceiptDescLong>Birdies Loyalty Redemption</RewardReceiptDescLong>
    </AddReward>""".rstrip()
        log(f"→ Offering prompted redemption: ${recommended_amount:.2f}")
    else:
        log("No redemption available/eligible for this cart.")

    # Customer greeting + reward actions
    return f"""
<GetRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <LoyaltyIDValidFlag value="yes">{masked}</LoyaltyIDValidFlag>
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>Welcome {first_name}!</DisplayLine>
        <DisplayLine>Points Balance: {points_balance}</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
  <RewardActions>{reward_actions_xml}
  </RewardActions>
</GetRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_finalize_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    global current_customer

    # Detect applied $ via tender/promotions
    applied_dollars = _detect_loyalty_tender_or_promos(root, REWARD_ID)
    if applied_dollars > 0:
        log(f"✓ Loyalty applied as tender: ${applied_dollars:.2f}")
        points_redeemed = int(round(applied_dollars * POINTS_PER_DOLLAR))
    else:
        log("ℹ No loyalty tender detected in finalize")
        points_redeemed = 0

    # Compute eligible subtotal (live lines only)
    eligible_subtotal = round(sum(it["amount"] for it in _only_live_item_lines(root)), 2)

    if not current_customer:
        log("⚠ No customer in session during Finalize; sending receipt lines only.")
        return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
      <ReceiptLine>Thank you for shopping at Birdies!</ReceiptLine>
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

    try:
        payload = {
            "customerId": current_customer.get("customerId"),
            "eligibleSubtotal": eligible_subtotal,
            "pointsRedeemed": points_redeemed,
            "transactionId": root.findtext(".//POSTransactionID") or root.findtext(".//TransactionID") or ""
        }
        r = SESSION.post(f"{BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/finalize-transaction", json=payload, timeout=REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
        if r.status_code == 200:
            res = r.json()
            points_earned = int(res.get("pointsEarned", 0))
            new_balance   = int(res.get("newBalance", 0))
            log(f"✓ Transaction finalized: Earned {points_earned} pts, New balance: {new_balance} pts")
            receipt = []
            if points_redeemed > 0:
                receipt.append(f"Points Redeemed: {points_redeemed} pts (${points_redeemed/POINTS_PER_DOLLAR:.2f})")
            receipt.append(f"Points Earned: {points_earned} pts")
            receipt.append(f"New Balance: {new_balance} pts")
            receipt.append("Thank you for shopping at Birdies!")
        else:
            log(f"⚠ Finalize POST failed: {r.status_code}")
            receipt = ["Thank you for shopping at Birdies!"]
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"⚠ Finalize error: {e}")
        receipt = ["Thank you for shopping at Birdies!"]
    finally:
        current_customer = None
        declined_rewards.clear()

    receipt_xml = "\n".join([f"      <ReceiptLine>{line}</ReceiptLine>" for line in receipt])
    return f"""
<FinalizeRewardsResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <ReceiptData>
{receipt_xml}
    </ReceiptData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</FinalizeRewardsResponse>""".strip()

def build_cancel_redemption_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    lrid = root.findtext(".//LoyaltyRewardID") or "UNKNOWN"
    declined_rewards.add(lrid)
    log(f"✗ Customer declined redemption: {lrid}")
    return f"<CancelRedemptionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelRedemptionResponse>"

def build_cancel_txn_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    log("Transaction cancelled; clearing session state.")
    current_customer = None
    declined_rewards.clear()
    return f"<CancelTransactionResponse>{resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}</CancelTransactionResponse>"

def build_get_customer_msg_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<GetCustomerMessagingResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <CustomerMessageData>
    <DisplayData>
      <DisplayCommand device="POS-Cashier" sequence="WhenReceived">
        <DisplayLine>Welcome to Birdies Loyalty!</DisplayLine>
      </DisplayCommand>
    </DisplayData>
  </CustomerMessageData>
</GetCustomerMessagingResponse>""".strip()

def build_end_period_response(root: ET.Element) -> str:
    pos_seq, loy_seq = get_req_ids(root)
    return f"""
<EndPeriodResponse>
  {resp_header(pos_seq, loy_seq)}
  <Result value="success"/>
</EndPeriodResponse>""".strip()

# =========================
# Connection Handler
# =========================
def handle_client(conn: socket.socket, addr):
    peer = f"{addr[0]}:{addr[1]}"
    log(f"POS connected from {peer}")

    if EXPECTED_POS_IP and addr[0] != EXPECTED_POS_IP:
        log(f"⚠ Rejecting unexpected POS IP: {addr[0]}")
        try: conn.close()
        except: pass
        return

    # POS-connected heartbeat
    send_heartbeat(addr[0])

    try:
        conn.settimeout(180)
        while True:
            hdr = recv_exact(conn, 28)
            if not hdr:
                log(f"POS disconnected: {peer}")
                break

            try:
                action, data_len, chk_data = parse_header(hdr)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"Bad header from {peer}: {e}")
                break

            if action == ACTION_HEARTBEAT:
                if data_len:
                    _ = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
                log(f"POS heartbeat from {peer}")
                continue

            data = recv_exact(conn, data_len)
            if len(data) != data_len or crc32(data) != chk_data:
                log(f"Payload CRC/length mismatch from {peer}")
                break

            try:
                root, _raw = parse_xml(data)
            except Exception as e:
                log(f"XML parse error from {peer}: {e}")
                break

            tag = root.tag.strip()

            if tag == "GetLoyaltyOnlineStatusRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_online_status_response(root))
                send_heartbeat(addr[0])

            elif tag == "GetRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_rewards_response(root))

            elif tag == "FinalizeRewardsRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_finalize_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelRedemptionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_redemption_response(root))

            elif tag == "CancelTransactionRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_cancel_txn_response(root))

            elif tag == "GetCustomerMessagingRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_get_customer_msg_response(root))

            elif tag == "EndPeriodRequest":
                send_xml(conn, build_end_period_response(root))

            elif tag in ("BeginCustomerRequest", "EndCustomerRequest"):
                log(f"{tag} received (no response required)")

            else:
                log(f"⚠ Unhandled message: {tag}")

    except socket.timeout:
        log(f"POS timeout: {peer}")
    except Exception as e:
        log(f"POS error: {peer} - {e}")
    finally:
        try:
            conn.close()
        except:
            pass
        log(f"Connection closed: {peer}")

# =========================
# Server
# =========================
def serve():
    log(f"Starting Birdies Loyalty Edge Agent")
    log(f"Store: {PDI_STORE_NUMBER} | POS Type: {POS_TYPE} | POS ID: {POS_ID}")
    log(f"Backend: {BACKEND_URL}")
    log(f"Listening on {HOST}:{PORT}")

    # Heartbeat thread
    threading.Thread(target=heartbeat_loop, daemon=True).start()
    log("✓ Heartbeat thread started")

    # POS listener
    s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    s.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    s.bind((HOST, PORT))
    s.listen(64)

    try:
        while True:
            conn, addr = s.accept()
            threading.Thread(target=handle_client, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        log("\nShutting down...")
    finally:
        s.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    serve()
What changed vs your last version

Instant $1 without prompt when eligible (identical to the demo’s on-wire XML).

Backend calls wrapped with tight timeouts and quick fallbacks so the POS never feels “stuck.”

Live-line parsing only for stable subtotals.

Finalize now logs the actual applied loyalty by reading Passport’s tender/promotions.

Minor cleanups (phone parsing, duplicate handler removal, clearer logs).

You said:
this still checks against the database though right?
ChatGPT said:
Yep—this version still checks your backend/database.

Where it happens: inside build_get_rewards_response

Customer lookup → POST {BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/customer-lookup (using phone or loyalty ID).

Redemption calc → POST {BACKEND_URL}/api/pos/calculate-redemption (given subtotal + items).
If the calc returns something ≥ $1, we clamp to $1.00 and send the instant basket discount.

What’s different (safer UX): backend calls use a tight timeout (2, 2) and are wrapped in try/except.

If lookup or calc fails or is slow, the agent replies immediately (no reward) so Passport doesn’t “spin.”

When the backend succeeds and customer has ≥100 pts, it sends the same on-wire XML as your local demo (instant, no prompt).

Finalize check: on FinalizeRewardsRequest we scan for your LoyaltyRewardID in TenderInfo / item Promotion to verify the $1 actually applied and compute points redeemed.

So: still DB-driven eligibility, but with instant apply and guardrails so the POS stays snappy.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 9s
Nice clean run — and your logs tell the story:

You did send the instant $1 basket reward at 12:28:09 (Instant=yes, line 0, amountOff=1.00). ✅

In FinalizeRewardsRequest (12:28:51) the only item is KIND bar with discountable="no". The tender line shows plain cash with no LoyaltyRewardID, and total tender is $2.75 (= $2.59 + $0.16 tax).

Your agent printed: “ℹ No loyalty tender detected in finalize” — which matches the payload.

What that means
Passport ignored the loyalty discount because the basket contained no discountable merchandise. When the only item(s) are discountable="no", Passport won’t create the loyalty tender for a transaction-level discount. (Earlier, when items were discountable="yes", we saw the loyalty tender appear.)

Quick way to verify
Try again with a discountable item (e.g., your Cup of Ice or Celsius can that shows discountable="yes"), then finalize. In Finalize you should see:

<TransactionLine>
  <TenderInfo>
    <TenderCode>loyaltyOffer</TenderCode>   (or equivalent)
    <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION</LoyaltyRewardID>
    <TenderAmount>1.00</TenderAmount>
  </TenderInfo>
</TransactionLine>
…and your agent will log the detected $1 tender.

Small improvement (edge code)
Add a guard so you don’t offer/auto-apply when no live discountable lines exist; instead show a friendly message:

After _only_live_item_lines(root), check if at least one item line is discountable="yes".

If none, return GetRewardsResponse with RewardActions/> and a display line like: “Loyalty not eligible on this item.”

Bottom line: your build and instant-apply path are correct; this particular transaction didn’t apply because the only item was non-discountable.

ChatGPT said:

Thought for 18s
Great run & super clear logs — thanks for pasting them.

Here’s what happened with the Celsius sale:

You did send the instant $1 basket reward correctly at 12:32:07 (Instant=yes, line 0, amountOff=1.00). ✅

The item was discountable="yes". ✅

In FinalizeRewardsRequest (12:32:51) the tender line shows plain cash with empty LoyaltyRewardID and a total of $3.38 (= $3.19 + $0.19 tax). There’s no loyalty tender in the payload. So the $1 was not applied.

Given the XML is correct and the item is discountable, this points to a POS configuration issue for basket (transaction-level) loyalty tenders, not your Edge code.

Why item promos worked earlier but basket didn’t
Your 2-for-$1 tests applied as item-level promotions (PromotionReason=loyaltyOffer) — that path doesn’t rely on a tender configuration.

A basket discount (line 0, amountOff) is posted by Passport as a loyalty tender. If the “loyalty tender” isn’t configured/enabled in Manager Workstation, Passport just… doesn’t create it — exactly what your Finalize shows (no LoyaltyRewardID under TenderInfo).

What to check in Passport MWS (one-time setup)
Tenders → (Create/Enable a loyalty tender)

Make sure a tender exists for the POSLoyalty interface (often surfaced as “loyalty offer” / “Loyalty Tender”).

Enable it for the lane, set allowable departments/categories, etc.

Ensure it’s permitted for non-fuel sales and is not restricted by min/max limits lower than $1.

Loyalty / POSLoyalty interface settings

Confirm the interface is online, and “transaction-level discounts/tender via loyalty” (wording varies by version) are enabled.

Some builds allow toggling whether loyalty discounts affect taxable base; but regardless of tax setting you should still see a tender line with a LoyaltyRewardID when it applies.

Quick sanity test after config

Ring a simple, discountable item (e.g., Celsius).

Enter loyalty.

Total out.

In Finalize, you should now see:

<TenderInfo>
  <TenderCode>...</TenderCode>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <TenderAmount>1.00</TenderAmount>
</TenderInfo>
Your Edge agent will flip from “ℹ No loyalty tender detected” to logging the detected $1.00.

If you need an immediate workaround
Until the tender is enabled, you can simulate the same net effect by sending an item-level reward instead of basket:

Target the live line (e.g., Celsius line number), and send:

RewardTargetLineNumber = <that line>
RewardDiscountMethod = amountOff
RewardValue = 1.00
RewardLimit type="quantity" = 1
This will show as an item promotion (which we know applies) and get you the $1 off right away. Once the tender is configured, switch back to the basket discount.

Bottom line: Your Edge code and on-wire XML are correct. The POS didn’t create the loyalty tender because the loyalty tender isn’t configured/allowed yet. Enable/allow the loyalty tender in MWS (and re-test), or use the item-level fallback in the meantime.

Thought for 14s
Yep—your demo server applied it. We can see it in Finalize:

TenderInfo shows TenderCode="loyaltyOffer" with
LoyaltyRewardID=DEMO-1OFF and TenderAmount=1. ✅

Then a normal cash tender for the remainder ($2.38).

That’s Passport’s proof the $1 basket discount was actually posted.

Why this worked while the Edge run didn’t earlier:

Item eligibility:
Your earlier Edge test used a non-discountable item (KIND bar). Basket loyalty won’t post if every item is discountable="no". When you moved to Celsius (discountable), it still didn’t post (read on).

Basket tender vs item promo:
The demo always sends a transaction-level (line 0) discount. Passport posts that as a tender. Item promos (like your 2-for) don’t need the tender config and will show up as Promotion lines instead.

Tiny but real gotcha: the Reward ID string.
Your demo uses a short LoyaltyRewardID="DEMO-1OFF"; Edge uses a long one (BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION). Some Passport builds are picky about this field (length/charset). If it can’t bind the loyalty tender to the reward, it may quietly skip posting the tender even when everything else is correct.

What to change so Edge matches the demo, 1-for-1:

Make the Reward ID short & simple (<= 16 chars, A–Z/0–9/–).
In Edge, set:

REWARD_ID = "BIRD-1OFF"
(or even "DEMO-1OFF" for a quick A/B.)

Stay instant, no prompt (you already did this).

Only send when there’s at least one discountable="yes" line.
(You’re logging items; just gate the reward on at least one discountable line.)

Re-test with a discountable item and complete the sale.
In Finalize you should now see:

<TenderInfo>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>BIRD-1OFF</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <TenderAmount>1.00</TenderAmount>
</TenderInfo>
If that shows up, Edge and the demo are functionally identical on-wire. If it still doesn’t, swap the Edge VENDOR_NAME to the demo’s (DemoLoyalty) for a single pass—rare, but a few sites tie tender allowances to the configured provider label.

Want me to paste the Edge snippet with the short REWARD_ID and a “has-discountable-line” guard baked in?

What’s actually different on-wire
From your logs, the only material wiring diffs are:

LoyaltyRewardID value

Demo uses a short ID: DEMO-1OFF (works).

Edge uses a long ID: BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION.
Some Passport builds are picky—overlong/complex IDs can fail tender posting silently.

VendorName (cosmetic, but worth A/B)

Demo: DemoLoyalty

Edge: BirdiesLoyalty

Everything else (Instant=yes, line 0, amountOff=1.00) is correct.

Fast A/B to prove it
In Edge, change:

REWARD_ID = "DEMO-1OFF"   # temporarily match the demo
Re-run a Celsius sale and finalize.

If you now see a loyalty tender in Finalize → the issue was the reward ID length/format.

Then pick a production-safe ID, e.g.:

REWARD_ID = "BIRD-1OFF"   # <= 12–16 chars, A–Z/0–9/-
and keep it that way.

If (rare) it still doesn’t post, do one more pass setting:

VENDOR_NAME = "DemoLoyalty"
and retry; if that works, you’ll want to mirror whatever MWS expects (you can switch the display name back later after verifying).

Optional safety in Edge
Before sending, ensure there’s at least one discountable="yes" line (you already hit that with Celsius). If none, don’t send the basket reward and log “not eligible.”

Bottom line: you did use Celsius with Edge, but Passport didn’t create the loyalty tender. Matching the short LoyaltyRewardID the demo uses should make Edge behave identically.

Likely spec conditions that block a basket tender (and how your runs line up)
Loyalty tender must be enabled/allowed in MWS

If it’s not, Passport silently won’t create the tender.

Demo worked → suggests tender is enabled. So this alone isn’t the culprit.

At least one eligible line must be discountable

KIND run: not discountable → no tender (expected).

Celsius run: discountable="yes" → still no tender (so something else is blocking).

LoyaltyRewardID format/length

Demo uses short ID DEMO-1OFF (posts tender).

Edge uses long ID BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION (tender never appears).

Many Passport builds are picky here (non-spec lengths/characters can cause silent ignore). Shorten to something like BIRD-1OFF.

Vendor identity alignment / MWS binding

Demo VendorName = DemoLoyalty (works).

Edge VendorName = BirdiesLoyalty.

Some site configurations bind the “loyalty tender” allowance or routing to a particular provider name/version. If MWS is set up for “DemoLoyalty” but you send “BirdiesLoyalty,” the tender path might not bind.

Message threading/IDs (sequence discipline)

Some versions expect the same LoyaltySequenceID to be echoed by the POS and the loyalty host across request→response→finalize. Your logs show different sequence IDs per cycle, which is normal, but ensure you always return a valid LoyaltySequenceID in GetRewardsResponse and FinalizeResponse and that POS sees it. (You’re already doing this; less likely the issue given demo works.)

Receipt text limits

Short ≤ 8 chars, long ≤ 24. You respect this in both.

Tax rules / non-merchandise

If all lines are tax-exempt or marked as a class blocked from loyalty tenders, Passport might suppress the tender. Your Celsius line is normal taxable merch, so unlikely.

Got it—I read the spec you uploaded and mapped it to your logs. Here’s the tight diagnosis, with the exact rules from the Passport POS/Loyalty spec and how your two runs line up.

What the spec says (relevant parts)
Transaction-level (basket) rewards: if you send RewardTargetLineNumber = 0, the POS must apply the discount to the entire transaction “as a POS-defined loyalty tender.” (See GetRewardsResponse / AddReward on page 12.) 
loyalty2


How that appears in Finalize: a basket loyalty discount shows up as a TransactionLine/TenderInfo with TenderCode = "loyaltyOffer", and the POS should echo your LoyaltyRewardID plus the TenderAmount. (See TenderInfo on page 27.) 
loyalty2


Item eligibility flag: each ItemLine has discountable="yes|no". If it’s no, the POS won’t apply promotions/discounts to that item. (See ItemLine on page 21.) 
loyalty2


Instant vs. prompt: InstantRewardFlag="yes" forces the reward; if "no", POS prompts the cashier using your prompt text. (See InstantRewardFlag on page 28.) 
loyalty2


Valid methods: for a transaction-level reward, only RewardDiscountMethod="amountOff" is valid—others are ignored. (See RewardDiscountMethod on page 29.) 
loyalty2


IDs/lengths: LoyaltyRewardID is a string up to 40 chars (no special character restrictions stated). (See Common Data Elements on page 28.) 
loyalty2


How that matches your two runs
Demo server (worked)
You sent basket reward with InstantRewardFlag="yes", line 0, amountOff=1.00.

In Finalize, the POS produced:

<TenderInfo>
  <TenderCode>loyaltyOffer</TenderCode>
  <LoyaltyRewardID>DEMO-1OFF</LoyaltyRewardID>
  <TenderAmount>1</TenderAmount>
</TenderInfo>
That is exactly what the spec describes for a transaction-level tender. ✔️ (pages 12 & 27) 
loyalty2


Edge agent (didn’t post the $1)
First attempt (KIND bar): discountable="no" → POS is allowed to not apply; your Finalize had no loyalty tender. That’s consistent. (page 21) 
loyalty2


Later attempt (Celsius, discountable="yes"): you sent the right XML (instant, line 0, amountOff=1.00), but Finalize still showed only a cash tender with empty LoyaltyRewardID—no loyaltyOffer line. That means the POS didn’t create the transaction-level tender this time, even though the request was valid by spec (pages 12, 27). 
loyalty2


Why the POS posted the demo tender but not the Edge tender
Per the spec, both messages are valid. So when the basket tender appears for Demo but not for Edge, the blockers are outside the XML fields you already matched:

POS/MWS configuration binding (most likely):
Basket tenders are a tender type in Passport. Stores often bind the “loyalty tender” to a specific provider configuration. If your tender is enabled/allowed for the Demo provider identity but not yet mapped for “BirdiesLoyalty”, Passport can accept your reward but skip creating the tender line. The spec doesn’t forbid this; it’s a configuration detail.

Provider / Reward ID quirk (site-specific):
The spec allows any LoyaltyRewardID up to 40 chars, but some site builds are picky in practice. Your demo used a short ID (DEMO-1OFF) and posted. Edge used a long ID (BIRDIES_POINTS_REDEMPTION). While the spec says this is fine, field experience says some setups only post tenders when the provider/ID matches the tender mapping the site is using.

What to do (sequence that matches the spec and your logs)
Verify the Passport tender mapping in Manager Workstation

Ensure a Loyalty tender type exists and is enabled for lanes.

Confirm it’s permitted on inside merchandise transactions.

If the tender is scoped by provider, add/enable it for BirdiesLoyalty as the vendor name shown in ResponseHeader. (Spec shows vendor names are carried—page 17—and many sites use them for mapping.) 
loyalty2


A/B the on-wire identity once

Temporarily set Edge REWARD_ID = "DEMO-1OFF" and (only if needed) VendorName="DemoLoyalty".

Ring Celsius and finalize. If the loyalty tender appears, switch to a production short ID like BIRD-1OFF and have your MWS mapping use BirdiesLoyalty going forward.

Keep your current XML rules (they’re spec-correct):

InstantRewardFlag="yes" (no prompt),

RewardTargetLineNumber=0,

RewardDiscountMethod="amountOff",

RewardValue=1.00.
That is precisely how a transaction-level loyalty tender is supposed to be triggered. (pages 12, 28–29) 
loyalty2


(Optional) Gate on eligibility to reduce confusion

Only send basket reward if there is at least one ItemLine discountable="yes" in the cart—so cashiers don’t expect a discount where the POS will (correctly) refuse. (page 21) 
loyalty2


Bottom line
Your Edge XML is compliant with the spec for a basket discount.

The demo shows Passport posting the tender exactly as defined.

When Edge’s tender didn’t appear—even on a discountable item—the most plausible cause is tender/provider configuration in Passport (plus, sometimes, stricter site rules around the VendorName/LoyaltyRewardID you send). Align those, and Edge will post the same loyaltyOffer tender you saw with the demo. 
loyalty2
